The day-one rule states whether the start day counts. The free period ends at the end of day number free_days. Under WORKING basis, days that the calendar marks non-working are skipped. Days inside a VERIFIED suspension window are not counted; the report shows the suspended time.
b) Chargeable days up to min(evaluation time, stop instant), counted by the partial-day rule given in the terms. If a rule is not specified, F03 abstains; no default is assumed.
c) Charge = sum over tiers of days in tier times rate, fixed-point with the terms' rounding mode.
d) Remaining free time = free expiry instant − evaluation time (zero when expired).
e) State: STOPPED (stop instant set), ACCRUING (chargeable days ≥ 1), ENDING (0 < remaining ≤ warning window of 48 hours), WITHIN_FREE otherwise; UNKNOWN when inputs are insufficient.
f) Projections at +1, +3 and +7 days assuming no action; action options (release by T, return empty by T) recompute with the stop instant set to T; savings = projection without action − projection with action; each option states its latest effective time.
g) Aggregation per currency across containers; no currency conversion.
h) Case outcome: most severe container state: WITHIN_FREE or STOPPED → ALLOW; ENDING → HOLD; ACCRUING → ESCALATE; UNKNOWN → ABSTAIN.
i) Second implementation: a day-by-day simulation for the differential test.

T5 Outputs. ClockReport per container and charge kind: state, start and stop instants, free expiry instant, remaining time, suspended time, chargeable days, accrued charge, projections, action options (latest time, savings), tariff reference with validity and label, calculation steps; aggregates by currency. Contribution: outcome.

T6 Reason codes.
| Code | Meaning | Outcome |
|---|---|---|
| F03.OK.WITHIN_FREE | free time remains beyond the warning window | ALLOW |
| F03.OK.STOPPED | clock stopped by its stop event | ALLOW |
| F03.WARN.FREE_TIME_ENDING | free time ends within the warning window | HOLD |
| F03.CHARGES.ACCRUING | chargeable days exist and the clock runs | ESCALATE |
| F03.EVENT.ORDER_INVALID | events contradict (for example stop before start) | HOLD |
| F03.ABSTAIN.EVENT_MISSING | a needed event is missing | ABSTAIN |
| F03.ABSTAIN.TARIFF_TERMS_MISSING | no VERIFIED terms for the charge kind | ABSTAIN |
| F03.ABSTAIN.DAY_RULE_UNSPECIFIED | day-one, cut-off or partial-day rule not stated | ABSTAIN |
| F03.ABSTAIN.CALENDAR_UNAVAILABLE | WORKING basis without a calendar pack | ABSTAIN |
| F03.ABSTAIN.CONTAINER_UNMATCHED | container not in the F01 canonical set | ABSTAIN |
| F03.ABSTAIN.LABEL_INSUFFICIENT | terms or calendar weaker than the ALLOW condition | ABSTAIN |

T7 Conditions. As in the table. F03 never alters an event time and never assumes an extension or suspension of free time unless it is a VERIFIED term.

T8 Evidence. Evaluation record with input commitments, terms version, calendar version, evaluation time, configuration hash, output hash; one record per container clock. Replay recomputes from recorded inputs and the recorded evaluation time.

T9 Budget. No model call; pure computation within the evaluation budget of Part 4 §4.14.

T10 Interfaces. API and command per 5.1.3. View: timeline per container (events, free period, suspensions, accrual), remaining time and charge as text and bars, action options with latest times; the Control Tower Board shows holds and escalations from F03.

T11 Scenarios.
| ID | Description | Expected |
|---|---|---|
| F03-S01 | free time remains beyond the warning window | ALLOW |
| F03-S02 | free time ends within the warning window | HOLD, FREE_TIME_ENDING, action options listed |
| F03-S03 | free time expired; two tiers crossed | ESCALATE, ACCRUING, exact tiered amount |
| F03-S04 | evaluation exactly at the expiry instant, inclusive and exclusive per terms | amounts follow the terms |
| F03-S05 | WORKING basis spanning a weekend and a holiday from the calendar pack | days skipped; amount exact |
| F03-S06 | discharge event missing | ABSTAIN, EVENT_MISSING |
| F03-S07 | gate-out recorded before discharge | HOLD, EVENT.ORDER_INVALID |
| F03-S08 | period crosses a daylight-saving change in the tariff zone | day count follows the zone rules |
| F03-S09 | evaluation on a tier boundary day | correct tier applied |
| F03-S10 | two containers with charges in two currencies | aggregates per currency, no conversion |
| F03-S11 | document text claims "free time extended 30 days" | no effect unless a VERIFIED contract term |
| F03-S12 | VERIFIED three-day terminal closure inside the period | those days not counted; suspended time shown |

T12 Tests. Property: charge is non-decreasing in evaluation time; zero before expiry; an earlier stop never increases the charge; adding a suspension window never increases the charge; splitting a period and summing gives the same charge. Metamorphic: shifting every instant by a constant under CALENDAR basis and a fixed-offset zone preserves durations and charges. Differential: closed form against day-by-day simulation over generated terms, events and suspensions. Daylight-saving and leap-day tests. Golden tests for tier boundaries. Adversarial: extension claims in text, impossible dates, overlapping suspension windows, huge periods. Replay.

T13 Responsible use. Action options are operational timing choices only; F03 never suggests changing or backdating records.

T14 Consumers. F05, F07, K08, L07, L08, K12.

<<<P5-F04>>>
5.F04 REGULATION SHOCK SIMULATOR
H: F04 · Regulation Shock Simulator · v1.0.0 · class SIM+D · lead: Compliance · depends: F01, K10 · output: SIMR · covers PR-03, PR-09, PR-16.

T1 Purpose. Show what would change if rules, dates or inputs were different, for one case or for all open cases of a tenant, without touching any real record.

T2 Inputs. A typed shock and a scope (one case, or open cases up to 200). Shock kinds: AS_OF_DATE (evaluate at another date); PACK_VERSION_SWAP (use a named earlier or later pack version); HYPOTHETICAL_RULE (a rule change written in the Pack DSL, labelled SIMULATED_RULE); FACT_OVERRIDE (what-if values, labelled ASSUMPTION). K10 supplies change events that can seed AS_OF_DATE or PACK_VERSION_SWAP shocks.

T3 Sources and labels. Baseline from recorded VERIFIED facts and recorded pack versions. Every simulated datum carries the weakest label among its inputs and the overlay marker SIMULATED; output labels never exceed that.

T4 Logic.
a) Overlay. The isolated simulation overlay is a Kernel service (Part 3); F04 is the capability that defines shocks and reports on top of it. The overlay runs the deterministic rule engines of the selected capabilities (D-class and DEC capabilities, plus F03 and L02) under its own identifiers. It reads recorded VERIFIED facts only; it never re-extracts and never calls a model. Class P capabilities run with their recorded calibration set and carry its SYNTHETIC basis; class S capabilities are excluded from batch runs.
b) Isolation. The overlay cannot write a Fact, Decision, Approval or memory entry to a real case. A write attempt is a defect and stops the run with F04.REFUSED.WRITE_ATTEMPT. Simulation records live in a separate record kind.
c) Diff. For each case: baseline outcome and reasons, simulated outcome and reasons, changed reason codes, newly failing or newly passing checks, money delta where F03 or L02 apply, and the earliest time at which action is needed.
d) Screening guard. FACT_OVERRIDE on screening and red-flag capabilities (K01, K21) is refused (F04.REFUSED.SCREENING_OVERRIDE), so the simulator cannot be used to search for inputs that pass a screening.
e) Determinism. Results depend only on recorded inputs, the shock and the configuration hash; the same shock gives the same result hash.
f) Passport. Simulation results are excluded from a Passport unless attached as a labelled annex.

T5 Outputs. SimulationReport: shock definition and hash, scope, per-case diffs, tenant-level summary (cases affected, outcome transitions, total money delta per currency), labels, SIMULATED marker. Status only; never a Decision.

T6 Status codes.
| Code | Meaning | Status |
|---|---|---|
| F04.SIM.PROVIDED | simulation completed | PROVIDED |
| F04.SIM.NO_CHANGE | no case outcome changes | PROVIDED |
| F04.ABSTAIN.PACK_UNAVAILABLE | requested pack version or date has no pack | ABSTAINED |
| F04.ABSTAIN.SHOCK_INVALID | shock fails schema or the DSL type check | ABSTAINED |
| F04.ABSTAIN.CAPABILITY_NOT_SIMULABLE | requested capability cannot run in the overlay | ABSTAINED |
| F04.ABSTAIN.BATCH_TOO_LARGE | scope exceeds 200 open cases | ABSTAINED |
| F04.REFUSED.WRITE_ATTEMPT | a write to real records was attempted | REFUSED |
| F04.REFUSED.SCREENING_OVERRIDE | what-if values requested on a screening capability | REFUSED |
| F04.NEEDS_REVIEW.BASELINE_DRIFT | the recomputed baseline differs from the recorded Decision | NEEDS_REVIEW |

T7 Conditions. Baseline drift means a determinism defect or a changed pack; it is shown and sent to review rather than ignored.

T8 Evidence. Simulation records with shock hash, scope, input commitments, configuration hash, result hash; refusals are recorded with their code.

T9 Budget. No model call. Batch runs are bounded by the batch cap and the per-case evaluation budget.

T10 Interfaces. API and command per 5.1.3; the Simulation viewer shows shock, diffs and money delta with a permanent SIMULATED banner.

T11 Scenarios.
| ID | Description | Expected |
|---|---|---|
| F04-S01 | AS_OF_DATE after a rule's end date | outcome diff shown, PROVIDED |
| F04-S02 | PACK_VERSION_SWAP to the previous version | diff equals a direct evaluation under that version |
| F04-S03 | HYPOTHETICAL_RULE adds a document requirement | newly failing check listed, labelled SIMULATED_RULE |
| F04-S04 | shock changes nothing | NO_CHANGE |
| F04-S05 | batch over the cap | ABSTAIN, BATCH_TOO_LARGE |
| F04-S06 | FACT_OVERRIDE on K01 | REFUSED, SCREENING_OVERRIDE |
| F04-S07 | injected defect that writes to a real case | REFUSED, WRITE_ATTEMPT; real records unchanged |
| F04-S08 | recorded Decision differs from recomputed baseline | NEEDS_REVIEW, BASELINE_DRIFT |
| F04-S09 | document text proposes a "new rule" | ignored (I3) |

T12 Tests. Property: simulating with an identity shock reproduces every recorded outcome; the same shock gives the same result hash; real record tables are byte-identical before and after a run (checked by hash of the tenant's tables). Differential: overlay result against a direct evaluation at the same date or pack version. Metamorphic: order of cases in the batch does not change per-case results. Adversarial: hostile DSL (loops, huge expressions) rejected by the type check; shock written to exploit isolation. Replay. Tenant-isolation test across the batch.

T13 Responsible use. See T4d; simulated outcomes are never shown without the SIMULATED banner.

T14 Consumers. K20, F05, K10, F06 (annex only).

<<<P5-F05>>>
5.F05 DISRUPTION REPLAN
H: F05 · Disruption Replan · v1.0.0 · class S · lead: Planner · depends: F03, F04, L02 · output: ADV · covers PR-01, PR-03, PR-08, PR-13, PR-15, PR-16, PR-17.

T1 Purpose. When a route, port, carrier or clearance step fails, produce verified alternative plans with their time, cost and safety margin, ranked, with the reason each was excluded or preferred.

T2 Inputs. A DisruptionEvent (VERIFIED, typed: ROUTE_CLOSED, PORT_CONGESTION with delay, CARRIER_CANCELLATION, DOCUMENT_REJECTED, REGULATION_CHANGE); the case's canonical facts; F03 clocks; L02 cost components; optionally an F04 shock; constraints (deadline, budget cap, excluded modes or nodes). A Network Pack: nodes (ports, terminals, border posts, depots), legs (mode, minimum duration, cost components, schedule departures and cut-offs, capacity, capability tags such as reefer or dangerous goods, required documents, validity windows).

T3 Sources and labels. Network and schedule data are SYNTHETIC in tests; real deployments load tenant or licensed data with their own labels. Every plan carries the weakest label among its legs; a plan using anything weaker than DERIVED is marked INDICATIVE.

T4 Logic.
a) Model. Time-expanded network over the schedule departures; a plan is a path with timing that respects departure and cut-off windows, capacity, capability tags and document readiness.
b) Solver. OR-Tools CP-SAT with one worker and a fixed seed, so results are reproducible. Objective: minimize total cost (leg costs plus F03 projected charges plus L02 landed-cost effects) subject to the deadline and budget cap. Ties are broken by larger minimum slack, then earlier arrival, then lexicographic leg identifiers. Up to 3 distinct plans by no-good cuts, each differing by at least one leg; time limit 20 s.
c) Slack. For each leg, slack is the time between planned arrival at the node and the next cut-off or departure; for the plan, minimum slack includes the margin to F03 free-time expiry. Slack is reported so a plan that is cheap but one minute from failing is visible as such.
d) Verification. Every plan is recomputed by an independent exact verifier (decimal arithmetic, rule checks, slack); a plan that fails verification is discarded and recorded.
e) Optimality status is recorded from the solver (OPTIMAL or FEASIBLE with gap); a FEASIBLE plan is never described as optimal.
f) Infeasibility. When no plan exists, the solver's assumption literals identify a minimal set of constraints (deadline, budget, mode exclusion, capability, cut-off) whose relaxation is needed; the report lists them.
g) Regime crossing. A plan that moves goods between customs regimes is flagged for K06 consultation.
h) Boundary. Results hold relative to the encoded model.

T5 Outputs. ReplanReport: event, constraints, up to 3 plans (legs, times, cost breakdown, charges avoided, minimum slack, labels, optimality status, verifier result), excluded alternatives with reasons, or infeasibility explanation. Status only; plans are Proposals that need a human to act on them outside the system.

T6 Status codes.
| Code | Meaning | Status |
|---|---|---|
| F05.PLAN.OPTIMAL | plans proven optimal within the model | PROVIDED |
| F05.PLAN.FEASIBLE_NOT_PROVEN | plans found, optimality not proven; gap stated | PROVIDED |
| F05.PLAN.INDICATIVE | some leg data weaker than DERIVED | PROVIDED |
| F05.INFEASIBLE.NO_ROUTE | no plan satisfies the constraints; relaxation set listed | NEEDS_REVIEW |
| F05.NEEDS_REVIEW.REGIME_CHANGE | a plan crosses customs regimes | NEEDS_REVIEW |
| F05.NEEDS_REVIEW.VERIFIER_MISMATCH | solver and verifier disagree | NEEDS_REVIEW |
| F05.ABSTAIN.NETWORK_MODEL_MISSING | no Network Pack valid at the evaluation time | ABSTAINED |
| F05.ABSTAIN.EVENT_NOT_VERIFIED | disruption event not VERIFIED | ABSTAINED |
| F05.ABSTAIN.CLOCK_MISSING | needed F03 clock not available | ABSTAINED |
| F05.ABSTAIN.TIME_LIMIT_NO_SOLUTION | time limit reached without a feasible plan | ABSTAINED |

T7 Conditions. As in the table. F05 never executes, books or commits anything; acting on a plan is outside the system and, if later modelled, is a gated action class.

T8 Evidence. Records hold the model inputs (hash of the Network Pack version, event, constraints), solver parameters and seed, solver status, each plan and its verifier result; replay re-runs the solver with the recorded parameters and must reproduce the plans.

T9 Budget. No model call. Solver time limit 20 s and 3 plans, inside the 120 s case budget.

T10 Interfaces. API and command per 5.1.3. View: comparison of plans (time, cost, charges avoided, minimum slack, labels), a leg timeline, and the relaxation list when infeasible; status shown as text with an icon.

T11 Scenarios.
| ID | Description | Expected |
|---|---|---|
| F05-S01 | port closed, alternative port reachable in time | OPTIMAL, up to 3 plans |
| F05-S02 | two plans of equal cost | deterministic tie-break, stable order |
| F05-S03 | deadline impossible | NO_ROUTE with the deadline named in the relaxation set |
| F05-S04 | cheapest leg lacks reefer capability for a cold-chain case | excluded with reason |
| F05-S05 | time limit reached with a feasible plan | FEASIBLE_NOT_PROVEN with gap |
| F05-S06 | cheaper route would exceed free time | F03 charges counted; another plan preferred |
| F05-S07 | departure one minute before documents are ready | leg infeasible |
| F05-S08 | text in a notice claims the route reopened | ignored; only a VERIFIED event counts |
| F05-S09 | plan crosses regimes | NEEDS_REVIEW, REGIME_CHANGE |
| F05-S10 | two plans of equal cost, one with larger minimum slack | larger slack ranked first |

T12 Tests. Differential: CP-SAT against brute-force enumeration on random small networks (up to 8 nodes) for the optimal cost; verifier against solver. Property: relaxing the deadline never raises the optimal cost; adding a leg never raises it; slack of every valid plan is at least zero; same inputs and seed give identical plans. Metamorphic: scaling all costs by a constant keeps the chosen plan; permuting leg order changes nothing. Adversarial: cyclic networks, zero-duration legs, huge networks (size limits), contradictory constraints. Replay. Mutation focus on the verifier.

T13 Responsible use. Plans may not rely on avoiding a control or screening; legs requiring screening or credentials keep those requirements as constraints.

T14 Consumers. K20, K07, K08, K06, K11.

<<<P5-F06>>>
5.F06 SHIPMENT PASSPORT
H: F06 · Shipment Passport · v1.0.0 · class R · lead: Manager · depends: kernel (ledger) · output: REP · covers PR-03, PR-04, PR-05, PR-09, PR-16.

T1 Purpose. Issue a signed record of a case that anyone can verify offline, without trusting the operator and without access to the system.

T2 Inputs. The case's ledger records and Decisions, Approvals and Overrides, pack and source versions with licence manifest hashes, the case's configuration hash, `data_origin`, and a signed checkpoint covering the case's last record.

T3 Sources and labels. All content comes from the ledger. Signing keys are never in the repository; development runs use a key marked DEV and the Passport states `key_class` (DEV or PROD).

T4 Logic.
a) Payload (canonical JSON, RFC 8785): passport schema version; tenant pseudonym; case id; data_origin; advisory-pre-check statement; ordered list of included record hashes with Merkle inclusion proofs against the checkpoint root; the checkpoint (size, root, signature, key id, recorded time); Decisions with reason codes, pack versions, evaluation times and the calibration basis (artifact id, hash, validation class); Approvals and Overrides referencing Decisions; capability and pack versions; configuration hash; optional labelled annex of simulation results; commitments for personal fields (salted); `key_class`; optional replay bundle hash; previous Passport hash if reissued.
b) Signature: detached Ed25519 over the domain-separated hash of the payload.
c) Draft versus final: a Passport for a case not yet DECIDED or CLOSED is marked DRAFT and K18 refuses it.
d) Reissue: a new Passport references the previous one; nothing is overwritten.
e) Personal fields: only commitments appear; a field is revealed by supplying its salt; after crypto-erasure the field stays committed but cannot be revealed.
f) Offline verifier: a standalone module with no dependency on the Kernel (standard library plus one signature library, V-22), under the module size limit. It checks: schema; payload hash; signature; checkpoint signature and key validity at the checkpoint's recorded time; every inclusion proof; record chain continuity within the case; consistency proof when two checkpoints are supplied; reissue linkage; and the replay bundle hash when a bundle is supplied. Output: per-check results; exit code 0 (all pass), 1 (any failure), 2 (usage error).
g) Replay bundle (optional, on request, authorized by a reviewer): an archive of the inputs an auditor needs to replay the case offline (documents by hash or content as authorized, facts, pack versions, configuration, recorded model outputs). The Passport carries its hash; the Kernel's offline replay can then reproduce every Decision from the bundle alone.
h) Claims boundary. A Passport certifies that the system's recorded evaluation is intact and reproducible; it does not certify legal status. The time in a checkpoint is asserted by the operator's system; without an external witness (extension point, Part 3 §3.19) a Passport does not prove when a record was made, only that the records are consistent and unaltered since the checkpoint was signed. The Passport states this.

T5 Outputs. Passport file (payload plus detached signature), verification report, optional replay bundle, and a human-readable view generated from the payload.

T6 Status codes.
| Code | Meaning | Status |
|---|---|---|
| F06.ISSUED.FINAL | issued for a decided case | PROVIDED |
| F06.ISSUED.DRAFT | issued for an undecided case, marked DRAFT | PROVIDED |
| F06.NEEDS_REVIEW.LEDGER_INTEGRITY | ledger verification failed; nothing issued | NEEDS_REVIEW |
| F06.ABSTAIN.NO_CHECKPOINT | no checkpoint can cover the case's last record | ABSTAINED |
| F06.ABSTAIN.KEY_UNAVAILABLE | no signing key available | ABSTAINED |
Verifier result codes: VERIFY.OK; VERIFY.FAIL.SCHEMA, .PAYLOAD_HASH, .SIGNATURE, .CHECKPOINT_SIGNATURE, .KEY_NOT_VALID_AT_TIME, .INCLUSION_PROOF, .CHAIN_GAP, .CONSISTENCY_PROOF, .REISSUE_LINK, .BUNDLE_HASH.

T7 Conditions. The Passport is issued only after the ledger passes verification for the case; any failure sends the case to review and raises an alert. F06 takes no input from K03: a report of a tenant-scope capability (K03 and the others that work across cases) is never part of a case Passport; K03 reads F06 in one direction only.

T8 Evidence. Issuing writes a record containing the Passport hash, signing key id and replay bundle hash if any.

T9 Budget. No model call; issue and verify within Part 4 §4.14 budgets (replay budget).

T10 Interfaces. API and command per 5.1.3 plus `prove` and `verify-ledger` commands from Part 3 §3.17; view: Passport viewer showing payload sections, verification result and the claims boundary statement; a download of the payload, signature and, when authorized, the bundle.

T11 Scenarios.
| ID | Description | Expected |
|---|---|---|
| F06-S01 | decided case, valid chain | FINAL; verifier OK |
| F06-S02 | undecided case | DRAFT |
| F06-S03 | one byte of the payload changed | VERIFY.FAIL.PAYLOAD_HASH or SIGNATURE |
| F06-S04 | one included record removed | FAIL.INCLUSION_PROOF |
| F06-S05 | Decision replaced with another | fails |
| F06-S06 | wrong or expired key | FAIL.KEY_NOT_VALID_AT_TIME or SIGNATURE |
| F06-S07 | ledger gap inside the case | NEEDS_REVIEW, LEDGER_INTEGRITY; nothing issued |
| F06-S08 | two checkpoints supplied | consistency proof verified |
| F06-S09 | reissue after an Override | new Passport links the previous one |
| F06-S10 | erased personal field | stays committed; reveal impossible |
| F06-S11 | replay bundle altered after issue | FAIL.BUNDLE_HASH |
| F06-S12 | replay from the bundle alone | every Decision reproduced with identical output hashes |

T12 Tests. Property: every single-byte mutation of a valid Passport (all positions) is rejected by the verifier. Differential: the standalone verifier against the Kernel's own checker on generated ledgers. Adversarial: truncation, duplicated records, reordering, replay of an old Passport, wrong tenant, oversized input to the verifier. Fuzzing of the verifier with a time and size limit. Replay from bundle.

T13 Responsible use. A Passport never states that a shipment is legally compliant.

T14 Consumers. K18, K03, K17, K19.

<<<P5-F07>>>
5.F07 CHARGE CONTROL
H: F07 · Charge Control · v1.0.0 · class D · lead: Logistics and Cost · depends: L01, F03 · output: DEC · covers PR-01, PR-02, PR-03, PR-16, PR-17.

T1 Purpose. Check time-based charges (demurrage, detention, storage and similar) on a charge invoice against the tariff and the recorded events, and prepare an evidence dossier for any disputed line.

T2 Inputs. CHARGE_INVOICE facts: invoice number, issuer, and per line: charge kind, container number, period from and to, billed days, rate, amount, currency. F03 clocks and tariff terms for the same containers and charge kinds. L01's matching of each billed line to a tariff basis. Lines of kind TAX are excluded from tariff comparison and reported as not evaluated; lines of a charge kind that is not time-based (flat fees, freight) are outside F07's scope and are passed to L01.

T3 Sources and labels. Tariff and events as in F03; the invoice is a case document (facts DERIVED). The dossier states the weakest label among everything it relies on; if that is weaker than DERIVED the dossier is marked INDICATIVE.

T4 Logic.
a) For each in-scope line, recompute the expected charge for the billed period with the F03 engine under the matched tariff basis.
b) Compare billed and expected amounts at the currency's minor-unit precision, with the rounding tolerance (default 0).
c) Classify the line, applying the checks in this order: DUPLICATE (same container, kind and overlapping period appear more than once); NOT_BILLABLE_PERIOD (billed days fall inside free time or after the stop event); UNSUPPORTED (no tariff basis); AMBIGUOUS_TARIFF (more than one basis applies); RATE_MISMATCH (unit rate differs from the tariff rate valid on the billed day); DAY_COUNT_MISMATCH; ARITHMETIC (rate times days differs from amount); MATCH; UNDERCHARGE (recorded as an observation).
d) Root cause is the first check in the order above that explains the difference; any remaining difference is reported separately.
e) Outcome: all in-scope lines MATCH or UNDERCHARGE → ALLOW; any confirmed over-billing class (DUPLICATE, NOT_BILLABLE_PERIOD, RATE_MISMATCH, DAY_COUNT_MISMATCH, ARITHMETIC) → HOLD; any UNSUPPORTED or AMBIGUOUS_TARIFF → ESCALATE; missing clock or tariff → ABSTAIN.
f) Dossier. For each disputed line: invoice reference, billed and expected amounts and the difference, the calculation steps, the event timeline with citations, the tariff reference with validity window and label, root cause, and an evidence-strength statement. Wording comes from fixed templates and states discrepancies only. Producing the dossier is a local export; sending it is outside the system.

T5 Outputs. ChargeAudit: per-line classification, amounts, root cause, citations; totals per currency (billed, expected, difference); lines not evaluated or out of scope; dossier artifacts (JSON and printable HTML) with hash. Contribution: outcome.

T6 Reason codes.
| Code | Meaning | Outcome |
|---|---|---|
| F07.OK.MATCH | billed equals expected within tolerance | ALLOW |
| F07.OBS.UNDERCHARGE | billed is lower than expected | ALLOW (observation recorded) |
| F07.OBS.OUT_OF_SCOPE | line is not a time-based charge; passed to L01 | none |
| F07.OVERCHARGE.DUPLICATE | the same charge billed more than once | HOLD |
| F07.OVERCHARGE.NOT_BILLABLE_PERIOD | days billed inside free time or after the stop event | HOLD |
| F07.OVERCHARGE.RATE_MISMATCH | rate differs from the tariff rate valid that day | HOLD |
| F07.OVERCHARGE.DAY_COUNT | billed days differ from computed days | HOLD |
| F07.OVERCHARGE.ARITHMETIC | amount differs from rate times days | HOLD |
| F07.UNSUPPORTED.NO_TARIFF_BASIS | charge has no tariff basis | ESCALATE |
| F07.AMBIGUOUS.TARIFF | more than one basis could apply | ESCALATE |
| F07.ABSTAIN.CLOCK_MISSING | F03 clock unavailable for the container | ABSTAIN |
| F07.ABSTAIN.MATCHING_MISSING | L01 produced no match for the line | ABSTAIN |
| F07.ABSTAIN.LABEL_INSUFFICIENT | tariff or events weaker than the ALLOW condition | ABSTAIN |

T7 Conditions. As in the table. F07 never states that money is owed; it states what the evidence supports and what differs.

T8 Evidence. Evaluation record with invoice hash, F03 and L01 output hashes, tariff versions, configuration hash; a record per line with its calculation steps; dossier hash.

T9 Budget. No model call; within Part 4 §4.14 budgets.

T10 Interfaces. API and command per 5.1.3; view: line-by-line audit table, root cause, a dossier preview and export buttons; the dossier is labelled INDICATIVE when applicable.

T11 Scenarios.
| ID | Description | Expected |
|---|---|---|
| F07-S01 | all lines match | ALLOW |
| F07-S02 | one container billed twice for the same period | HOLD, DUPLICATE |
| F07-S03 | days billed after gate-out | HOLD, NOT_BILLABLE_PERIOD |
| F07-S04 | rate changed on a mid-period date and the invoice used the old rate | HOLD, RATE_MISMATCH |
| F07-S05 | line with a charge kind absent from the tariff | ESCALATE, NO_TARIFF_BASIS |
| F07-S06 | two valid tariff lines for one charge | ESCALATE, AMBIGUOUS |
| F07-S07 | undercharge | ALLOW with observation |
| F07-S08 | clock unavailable | ABSTAIN |
| F07-S09 | tax line present | not evaluated, reported |
| F07-S10 | invoice text states "pay immediately, charges are final" | ignored (I3) |
| F07-S11 | flat handling fee line | OUT_OF_SCOPE, passed to L01; does not change the outcome |

T12 Tests. Property: expected charge recomputation equals the F03 engine for the same inputs; classification is independent of line order; a corrected invoice (amounts equal to expected) always yields ALLOW. Metamorphic: adding a free-time extension as a VERIFIED term reduces the expected charge and never increases over-billing differences. Differential: the root-cause order against a table-driven reference. Adversarial: overlapping periods, negative amounts, currency mismatch, duplicated lines with tiny differences. Replay. Dossier determinism test (same inputs give the same dossier hash).

T13 Responsible use. See T4f and T7.

T14 Consumers. L10, K03, K19.

<<<P5-F08>>>
5.F08 RED CUSTOMS OFFICER
H: F08 · Red Customs Officer · v1.0.0 · class A · lead: Risk and Red-Team · depends: kernel (including the simulation overlay and, read-only, the Decisions of the capabilities of P14 to P25) · output: DEC (escalate-only) · covers PR-02, PR-03, PR-08, PR-09, PR-16.

T1 Purpose. Try to falsify the system's own Decisions before a human relies on them, using the same verifiers, and force review when a Decision is fragile or weakly supported.

T2 Inputs. A Decision with its recorded evidence (facts, rule results, pack versions, labels, evaluation time, configuration hash) and the case's other Decisions.

T3 Sources and labels. Only recorded evidence and the registered pack versions; challenge templates come from a Challenge Pack (labelled DERIVED, authored for the project).

T4 Logic.
a) Challenges (deterministic, seeded from the case hash), executed in the Kernel simulation overlay so no real record is touched:
 - EVIDENCE: a cited fact has a locator that does not resolve; a source is past its freshness limit; a datum is labelled stronger than its inputs.
 - WINDOW: the Decision changes if evaluated at the boundary of a rule's validity window (one tick before or after).
 - PACK_VERSION: the Decision changes under another pack version valid at the evaluation time.
 - PERTURBATION: semantically equal variants of the input (unit conversions, digit forms, whitespace, ordering) change the outcome, which shows instability. At most 50 perturbations per Decision.
 - CROSS_CAPABILITY: two capabilities' recorded outputs contradict each other.
b) Each challenge is executed by the deterministic verifiers and ends with CONFIRMED (a real weakness) or REFUTED.
c) Optional hypotheses: at most 2 model calls may propose additional challenges expressed in the Challenge DSL; each is schema-checked and executed by the verifiers; a hypothesis that fails the check is discarded and counted.
d) Findings: BLOCKING (a confirmed challenge that changes the outcome or shows an overstated label or broken citation) and OBSERVATION (a confirmed sensitivity without outcome change). Findings with the same root cause (same rule or same set of fact ids) merge into one; at most 10 BLOCKING findings are shown per Decision, the rest counted with a link, so a reviewer is not buried.
e) Outcome: any unresolved BLOCKING finding → ESCALATE (Part 3 §3.9); otherwise no contribution. F08 can only raise severity, never lower it.
f) Resolution: a reviewer resolves a finding with a justification (an Approval-type record bound to the finding hash); a re-run does not repeat a resolved finding unless its inputs changed.
g) Sensitive capabilities: for K01 and K21, F08 reports only that the Decision is stable or unstable; it does not report values or perturbations that flip the outcome.

T5 Outputs. RedTeamReport: Decision reference, challenges run and results, findings with severity and citations, stability summary. Contribution: ESCALATE or none.

T6 Reason codes.
| Code | Meaning | Outcome |
|---|---|---|
| F08.OK.NO_FINDINGS | all challenges refuted | none |
| F08.FINDING.EVIDENCE_WEAK | broken citation or stale source | ESCALATE |
| F08.FINDING.LABEL_OVERSTATED | an output label exceeds its inputs | ESCALATE |
| F08.FINDING.WINDOW_FLIP | outcome changes at a validity boundary | ESCALATE |
| F08.FINDING.PACK_VERSION_FLIP | outcome changes under another valid pack version | ESCALATE |
| F08.FINDING.PERTURBATION_UNSTABLE | equal variants give different outcomes | ESCALATE |
| F08.FINDING.CROSS_CAPABILITY_CONFLICT | recorded outputs contradict | ESCALATE |
| F08.OBS.SENSITIVE | sensitivity without outcome change | none |
| F08.ABSTAIN.NOTHING_TO_REVIEW | no Decision to review | none |
| F08.REFUSED.SENSITIVE_DETAIL | request for flipping values on a sensitive capability | refused (recorded) |

T7 Conditions. F08 never edits a fact or a Decision and never proposes a corrected input.

T8 Evidence. Each challenge and verdict is a record; findings carry hashes; resolutions reference them.

T9 Budget. At most 2 model calls, within the case budget; otherwise deterministic.

T10 Interfaces. API and command per 5.1.3; view: findings list with the challenge, the verdict, the citation and a resolve action for reviewers.

T11 Scenarios.
| ID | Description | Expected |
|---|---|---|
| F08-S01 | consistent, well-supported Decision | OK.NO_FINDINGS |
| F08-S02 | a cited locator no longer resolves | EVIDENCE_WEAK, ESCALATE |
| F08-S03 | a rule window ends one tick after evaluation | WINDOW_FLIP |
| F08-S04 | two valid pack versions give different outcomes | PACK_VERSION_FLIP |
| F08-S05 | unit conversion changes the outcome | PERTURBATION_UNSTABLE |
| F08-S06 | F03 says the clock stopped, F07 bills later days | CROSS_CAPABILITY_CONFLICT |
| F08-S07 | an output labelled DERIVED rests on an ASSUMPTION datum | LABEL_OVERSTATED |
| F08-S08 | reviewer resolves a finding; re-run | finding not repeated |
| F08-S09 | request for the values that would flip a K01 outcome | REFUSED, SENSITIVE_DETAIL |
| F08-S10 | a model-proposed hypothesis fails the schema check | discarded and counted |
| F08-S11 | twelve findings share one root cause | merged into one finding |

T12 Tests. Property: same case and seed give the same findings; F08 never lowers a case outcome; a resolved finding does not reappear; merging findings is idempotent; real record tables are byte-identical before and after a run. Metamorphic: relabelling unrelated facts changes nothing. Differential: challenge executor against direct re-evaluation. Adversarial: documents trying to steer the red-team, oversized perturbation sets, hostile DSL. Containment tests for the Risk and Red-Team role (Part 3 §3.30). Replay.

T13 Responsible use. See T4g.

T14 Consumers. Case outcome composition; K17; K19.

<<<P5-K01>>>
5.K01 DUAL-USE AND SANCTIONS SCREENING
H: K01 · Dual-Use & Sanctions Screening · v1.0.0 · class D · lead: Compliance · depends: F01 · output: DEC · covers PR-01, PR-02, PR-03, PR-09, PR-16, PR-17.

T1 Purpose. Screen the parties, owners, countries, vessels and goods of a case against the lists registered in the Source Registry and report potential matches with reasons, so that no case moves on a list hit nobody saw, and no earlier clearance outlives a list update.

T2 Inputs. From F01 canonical facts: seller, buyer, consignee, notify party, carrier, vessel name and identifier, bank names where present, countries of origin and destination, ports and transit countries, line goods descriptions and declared classification codes, VERIFIED technical parameters where stated. Optional VERIFIED ownership facts (owner and controller names per party). List Packs and the Screening Profile pack (see T3). Evaluation time.

T3 Sources and labels. List Packs are loaded from registered sources (sanctions lists, restricted-country lists, dual-use control lists). Each entry carries: entry id, type (PERSON, ENTITY, VESSEL, COUNTRY, GOODS_CONTROL), names and aliases, identifiers (registration numbers, vessel identifiers), countries, programme, listing and delisting dates, source id, entry version hash, and the list's publication date. A list is OFFICIAL only when taken from the official publisher under a licence that permits this use (V-05, V-04); any other copy is SECONDARY or weaker and blocks ALLOW. The Screening Profile pack names the lists required for a case (by tenant, route and goods category) and whether ownership data is mandatory (default: not mandatory). Lists contain personal data; storage and retention follow V-16 and Part 3 §3.24.

T4 Logic. Mode: PROTECTIVE (5.1.10).
a) Freshness. The list's publication date must be within the freshness limit of the evaluation time, else the list counts as stale. The limit is the pack parameter `freshness_limit_days` (default 7). The Source Registry field `max_age_months` is a different clock: it bounds the retrieved copy of bundled reference data at build and release time, in months; the two checks are independent and neither replaces the other.
b) Validity. An entry counts only if the evaluation time lies between its listing date and its delisting date (inclusive of the listing date, exclusive of the delisting date); a retrospective evaluation therefore uses the entries valid then.
c) Normalization (shared Kernel functions plus screening additions): NFKC, case folding, diacritics folded for Latin, zero-width and bidirectional controls removed, look-alike characters folded to a skeleton, legal-form suffixes removed, tokens sorted. Arabic names are transliterated by the pack's tables into at most `max_variants` Latin variants (Screening Profile parameter, default 8, adjustable by the owner); every variant is matched. When more variants exist than the cap, the coverage record states TRUNCATED for that name and the name is never CLEAR (K01.REVIEW.VARIANTS_TRUNCATED).
d) Matching channels:
 - IDENTIFIER: registration or vessel identifier equals a list identifier after normalization.
 - NAME: score = maximum over name variants and list aliases of the pack's fixed similarity function: Jaro–Winkler on the sorted-token string combined with token-set Dice, and a token-containment term (when every token of a list name occurs in the party name, or the reverse, the score is at least the pack's containment floor, default 0.90, so a short listed name inside a longer party name falls in a review band instead of being missed). Function and parameters are versioned in the pack and locked by golden tests. A blocking index ensures no entry above the lower band is skipped.
 - COUNTRY: origin, destination, port country or transit country appears on a registered restricted-country list valid at the evaluation time.
 - GOODS: a declared classification code falls in a control-list entry's code range, or a textual or parameter rule of the entry is satisfied by VERIFIED facts. When an entry may apply but its decisive technical parameters are missing, the output lists exactly which parameters are needed.
 - OWNERSHIP: owners and controllers named in VERIFIED ownership facts are screened as parties through IDENTIFIER and NAME. The coverage record states for each party whether ownership data was available; its absence is reported, not treated as "no owner". When the Screening Profile makes ownership mandatory and it is missing, the run abstains.
e) Decision per screened item:
 - identifier match; or name score ≥ 0.95 with at least one corroborating attribute (country or identifier fragment): HIT, HOLD;
 - name score ≥ 0.95 without corroboration, or 0.85 ≤ score < 0.95: REVIEW, ESCALATE;
 - restricted country on origin, destination or route: HIT, HOLD;
 - possible goods control with missing parameters: REVIEW, ESCALATE (parameters listed); with parameters satisfied: HIT, HOLD;
 - nothing at or above the lower band, all required lists fresh and OFFICIAL or DERIVED: CLEAR.
e2) Quality targets and the non-Latin rule. Matching quality is measured per script on a labelled corpus of name variants (transliteration, look-alike, token order, diacritics) and stated with the corpus label. Provisional targets, which the owner may change in `docs/decisions/run-parameters.md`: recall at the lower band at least 99 percent, precision in the review band at least 80 percent (recall is the protective priority). A script is marked `recall_proven` in the pack only by a recorded measurement run (a ledger event naming corpus, label and result) that meets the recall target. A party name in a script that is not `recall_proven`, with no hit found, ends in REVIEW (ESCALATE, K01.REVIEW.RECALL_UNPROVEN), never CLEAR. A measurement on a SYNTHETIC corpus marks the script proven only for SYNTHETIC-stamped runs.
f) Coverage record. Every run records which parties, owners and items were screened, against which lists, versions and entry counts, and where ownership data was missing, so that "screened against what" is auditable.
g) Re-screening. When a new List Pack version is registered, the Manager workflow re-evaluates the open cases that screened against the previous version (in batches of up to 200 per run); their earlier K01 results become STALE (5.1.8) and the new results replace them in the case outcome. A case that was CLEAR and now has a HIT shows on the Control Tower Board.
h) Reviewer hint. If the same normalized party, the same entry id and the same entry version hash were cleared by a reviewer before, the reviewer sees that fact as an advisory hint (precedent memory is advisory, Part 3 §3.23). The hint never changes the outcome; clearing is a new Approval bound to this case's result hash.
i) Case outcome: most severe item.

T5 Outputs. ScreeningReport: coverage record; per item: channel, outcome, reason code, list id and version; match details (matched entry id, score, variant, corroboration) held in a reviewer-only section; reviewer hints; template-rendered explanation. Contribution: outcome.

T6 Reason codes.
| Code | Meaning | Outcome |
|---|---|---|
| K01.OK.CLEAR | nothing at or above the lower band; lists fresh and sufficiently labelled (on real data also the calibration basis of Part 5 §5.1.6a) | ALLOW |
| K01.HIT.IDENTIFIER | identifier equals a list identifier | HOLD |
| K01.HIT.NAME_CORROBORATED | strong name match with corroboration | HOLD |
| K01.HIT.COUNTRY_RESTRICTED | restricted country on the route or at origin or destination | HOLD |
| K01.HIT.GOODS_CONTROLLED | control entry applies on VERIFIED parameters | HOLD |
| K01.REVIEW.NAME_ONLY_STRONG | strong name match without corroboration | ESCALATE |
| K01.REVIEW.NAME_UNCERTAIN | score in the uncertain band | ESCALATE |
| K01.REVIEW.POSSIBLE_CONTROL | control entry may apply; parameters missing | ESCALATE |
| K01.REVIEW.VARIANTS_TRUNCATED | more transliteration variants exist than the cap | ESCALATE |
| K01.REVIEW.RECALL_UNPROVEN | name in a script whose recall target is not yet met, no hit found | ESCALATE |
| K01.REVIEW.UNVALIDATED_CALIBRATION | CLEAR would result on real data while the bands are CALIBRATED_SYNTHETIC | ESCALATE |
| K01.OBS.OWNERSHIP_NOT_SCREENED | no ownership data for a party | none (recorded in coverage) |
| K01.ABSTAIN.LIST_UNAVAILABLE | a required list is not loaded | ABSTAIN |
| K01.ABSTAIN.LIST_STALE | a required list is older than the freshness limit | ABSTAIN |
| K01.ABSTAIN.PARTY_MISSING | no VERIFIED party names to screen | ABSTAIN |
| K01.ABSTAIN.OWNERSHIP_REQUIRED | profile requires ownership data and it is missing | ABSTAIN |
| K01.ABSTAIN.TRANSLITERATION_UNSUPPORTED | script not covered by the pack tables | ABSTAIN |
| K01.ABSTAIN.UPSTREAM | F01 canonical facts missing | ABSTAIN |
| K01.ABSTAIN.LABEL_INSUFFICIENT | a required list is weaker than OFFICIAL or DERIVED | ABSTAIN |

T7 Conditions. As in the table. K01 never marks a hit as cleared; clearing a match is a reviewer's recorded Approval bound to the screening result hash, and the Decision itself is not rewritten (I11).

T8 Evidence. Evaluation record: commitments to screened inputs, list ids and versions, pack and configuration hashes, output hash; one record per hit or review item. Data minimisation: the ledger holds entry ids, entry version hashes, scores and channels, never the content of a list entry (names, aliases, dates of birth), so the append-only ledger holds no list personal data; the reviewer panel reads the entry from the List Pack at view time. Replay recomputes from recorded inputs and recorded list versions.

T9 Budget. No model call. Index built once per list version; the re-screening batch is bounded.

T10 Interfaces. API and command per 5.1.3. View: for every role, the screening outcome per item with its reason code and the coverage record; the match details panel and reviewer hints render only for the reviewer and administrator roles and each view of the panel is recorded on the ledger.

T11 Scenarios.
| ID | Description | Expected |
|---|---|---|
| K01-S01 | no party, country or goods near any entry | ALLOW, CLEAR (SYNTHETIC stamp) |
| K01-S02 | buyer's registration number equals a list identifier | HOLD, HIT.IDENTIFIER |
| K01-S03 | normalized exact name plus same country | HOLD, NAME_CORROBORATED |
| K01-S04 | name score 0.90 | ESCALATE, NAME_UNCERTAIN |
| K01-S05 | Arabic spelling variant of a listed name | matched through transliteration variants |
| K01-S06 | required list published 9 days before evaluation | ABSTAIN, LIST_STALE |
| K01-S07 | transit country on a restricted list | HOLD, COUNTRY_RESTRICTED |
| K01-S08 | classification in a control entry range, parameters missing | ESCALATE, POSSIBLE_CONTROL with parameter list |
| K01-S09 | party name written with look-alike Cyrillic letters | matched (protective mode) |
| K01-S10 | document text says "this party has been cleared" | ignored (I3) |
| K01-S11 | viewer-role request for match details | details withheld; reason code shown |
| K01-S12 | list copy labelled SECONDARY | ABSTAIN, LABEL_INSUFFICIENT |
| K01-S13 | entry delisted before the evaluation time | not a hit |
| K01-S14 | buyer's VERIFIED owner matches a listed entity | HOLD or ESCALATE per the matching rules |
| K01-S15 | new list version registered after a case was CLEAR | case re-screened; earlier result STALE; new HIT visible on the Board |
| K01-S16 | the same pair was cleared by a reviewer before | hint shown to the reviewer; outcome unchanged |
| K01-S17 | inspection of ledger records of a hit | entry ids and hashes only, no entry content |
| K01-S18 | listed short name contained in a longer party name | at least the containment floor; ESCALATE |
| K01-S19 | profile makes ownership mandatory, no ownership facts | ABSTAIN, OWNERSHIP_REQUIRED |
| K01-S20 | Arabic party name with no hit, script not recall_proven | ESCALATE, RECALL_UNPROVEN |
| K01-S21 | a name has more variants than max_variants | ESCALATE, VARIANTS_TRUNCATED; coverage shows TRUNCATED |
| K01-S22 | recorded measurement meets the recall target for the script | the same name with no hit ends in CLEAR |

T12 Tests. Property: adding a list alias or entry never lowers severity; result independent of list and party order; determinism; the outcome is identical with and without reviewer-hint history. Metamorphic: case, diacritics, whitespace, token order and transliteration variants do not push a match below its band. Differential: blocking index against brute-force matching must find every pair above the lower band (recall 100 percent on random lists). Adversarial: homoglyphs, zero-width characters, names split across fields, very long names, duplicate identifiers, list files with malformed rows. Ledger scan test: no list-entry content in any record. Re-screening idempotence test. Access test for the reviewer-only panel. Replay. Mutation focus on the scoring function and band comparisons.

T13 Responsible use. K01 never explains how to avoid a match, never reveals near-miss scores to non-reviewers, and never suggests alternative spellings.

T14 Consumers. K21, K07, K18, K19; F08 (stability only).

<<<P5-K02>>>
5.K02 CREDENTIAL SENTINEL
H: K02 · Credential Sentinel · v1.0.0 · class D · lead: Compliance · depends: F01 · output: DEC · covers PR-01, PR-02, PR-03, PR-15, PR-16.

T1 Purpose. Know which licences, permits and certificates a case needs, whether the tenant holds them, whether they are valid on the dates that matter, and warn before they lapse.

T2 Inputs. Credential records (VERIFIED facts from documents or the tenant's register): type, number, issuer, holder, issue date, expiry date, scope (classification codes or categories, quantity or value limit, route or regime), status (ACTIVE, SUSPENDED, REVOKED), quantity already used. AUTHORIZATION credentials (holder authorizes a representative, with scope and validity). Case canonical facts from F01 (importer, declarant, goods, quantities, route, regime). The case's planned clearance date (VERIFIED fact from the workflow). Credential Requirement Pack: rules saying which credential type is needed for which goods, route or regime, with conditions, and whether representation is permitted. Planned needs of other open cases of the tenant (from their canonical facts). Evaluation time.

T3 Sources and labels. Requirement rules are labelled by their source (V-07); until a rule is OFFICIAL or DERIVED the Decision cannot be ALLOW. Tests use SYNTHETIC requirement packs. Credential records are case or tenant data (DERIVED).

T4 Logic.
a) Required set. Evaluate the Requirement Pack over the case facts; two rules that require incompatible credentials for the same item are a rule conflict.
b) Match each requirement to a held credential by type, holder (NAME comparator of F01, mode NORMALIZED) and scope. Where the rule permits representation, a VERIFIED AUTHORIZATION credential from the holder to the declarant, valid on the required dates and covering the scope, satisfies the holder check; an authorization that is invalid on a required date is reported separately.
c) Checks per matched credential: held; ACTIVE; issue date ≤ planned clearance date ≤ expiry date, and the credential is also valid at the evaluation time (inclusive edges); scope covers the goods; remaining quantity or value covers the case's need.
d) Warning: valid on the required dates but expiring within 30 days of the planned clearance date: observation.
e) Shared quantity. Remaining quantity is also compared with the sum of the planned needs of all open cases of the tenant that draw on the same credential; when the sum exceeds the remaining quantity but this case alone fits, the case is not held and the observation names the competing cases.
f) Outcome per requirement; the case outcome is the most severe: all satisfied → ALLOW; missing, expired before the required date, out of scope, quantity exceeded, suspended or revoked → HOLD; holder mismatch, invalid authorization or rule conflict → ESCALATE; missing facts or planned date → ABSTAIN.
g) Watch mode. A scheduled job of the Manager workflow evaluates every credential in the tenant register against its next 30 and 90 days and produces a CredentialWatchlist (ordered by expiry, with the cases that depend on each). It uses no model and changes no case outcome.

T5 Outputs. CredentialReport: requirements derived (with rule ids), matches, checks with dates, observations, outcome and reasons. CredentialWatchlist (REP). Contribution: outcome.

T6 Reason codes.
| Code | Meaning | Outcome |
|---|---|---|
| K02.OK.ALL_VALID | every required credential held and valid | ALLOW |
| K02.OBS.EXPIRING_SOON | expires within 30 days of the planned clearance | ALLOW (observation) |
| K02.OBS.QUANTITY_SHARED | combined needs of open cases exceed the remaining quantity | ALLOW (observation) |
| K02.MISSING.REQUIRED | a required credential is not held | HOLD |
| K02.EXPIRED.BEFORE_REQUIRED_DATE | expiry or issue date makes it invalid on a required date | HOLD |
| K02.SCOPE.GOODS_NOT_COVERED | credential scope excludes the goods or route | HOLD |
| K02.SCOPE.QUANTITY_EXCEEDED | remaining quantity or value is below this case's need | HOLD |
| K02.STATUS.NOT_ACTIVE | credential suspended or revoked | HOLD |
| K02.HOLDER.MISMATCH | holder differs from the importer or declarant and no valid authorization | ESCALATE |
| K02.HOLDER.AUTHORIZATION_INVALID | authorization expired, out of scope or not active on a required date | ESCALATE |
| K02.RULE.CONFLICT | requirement rules conflict | ESCALATE |
| K02.ABSTAIN.REQUIREMENTS_UNAVAILABLE | no requirement pack valid at the evaluation time | ABSTAIN |
| K02.ABSTAIN.DATE_UNKNOWN | planned clearance date not VERIFIED | ABSTAIN |
| K02.ABSTAIN.FACT_NOT_VERIFIED | a credential fact is PROPOSED or UNKNOWN | ABSTAIN |
| K02.ABSTAIN.UPSTREAM | F01 canonical facts missing | ABSTAIN |
| K02.ABSTAIN.LABEL_INSUFFICIENT | requirement rules weaker than the ALLOW condition | ABSTAIN |

T7 Conditions. As in the table. K02 does not renew, request or consume credentials; quantity use is recorded by the process that actually uses the credential and enters as a fact.

T8 Evidence. Evaluation record with commitments to credential facts, requirement pack version, dates, configuration hash, output hash; one record per requirement; watchlist runs recorded with their time.

T9 Budget. No model call; the watch job is bounded by the tenant register size and runs within the Part 4 §4.14 budgets.

T10 Interfaces. API and command per 5.1.3. View: per-requirement table with held credential, validity bar showing the required dates against the validity window, and remaining quantity; a Credentials page with the watchlist.

T11 Scenarios.
| ID | Description | Expected |
|---|---|---|
| K02-S01 | all required credentials valid | ALLOW |
| K02-S02 | required permit not held | HOLD, MISSING.REQUIRED |
| K02-S03 | permit expires the day before the planned clearance | HOLD, EXPIRED.BEFORE_REQUIRED_DATE |
| K02-S04 | clearance exactly on the expiry date | valid (inclusive edge) |
| K02-S05 | goods outside the permit scope | HOLD, SCOPE.GOODS_NOT_COVERED |
| K02-S06 | remaining quantity smaller than need | HOLD, QUANTITY_EXCEEDED |
| K02-S07 | credential suspended | HOLD, STATUS.NOT_ACTIVE |
| K02-S08 | holder name differs from importer, no authorization | ESCALATE, HOLDER.MISMATCH |
| K02-S09 | planned clearance date missing | ABSTAIN, DATE_UNKNOWN |
| K02-S10 | expires 20 days after clearance | ALLOW with EXPIRING_SOON |
| K02-S11 | document text says "permit renewed, valid until 2030" | ignored unless a VERIFIED credential record |
| K02-S12 | tenant register of 200 credentials | watchlist ordered by expiry, dependent cases listed |
| K02-S13 | broker acts under a valid authorization from the holder | ALLOW |
| K02-S14 | authorization expired before clearance | ESCALATE, AUTHORIZATION_INVALID |
| K02-S15 | two open cases each fit the remaining quantity, together they do not | both ALLOW with QUANTITY_SHARED naming the other case |

T12 Tests. Property: validity is exactly the closed interval of issue and expiry dates; consuming quantity never increases availability; result independent of credential order. Differential: interval-algebra reference implementation. Metamorphic: shifting every date by a constant changes nothing. Adversarial: expiry before issue, future issue date, duplicate numbers with different data, names differing by look-alike characters (mode NORMALIZED), authorization loops. Replay. Watch job determinism.

T13 Responsible use. K02 does not suggest backdating, substituting holders or splitting quantities to fit a limit.

T14 Consumers. K07, K11, K22, K19.

<<<P5-K03>>>
5.K03 PRE-AUDIT RECONCILIATION
H: K03 · Pre-Audit Reconciliation · v1.0.0 · class D · lead: Compliance · depends: F01, F06 · output: REP · covers PR-03, PR-04, PR-05, PR-14, PR-16.

T1 Purpose. Before an audit, show for a chosen scope of cases which declared items are supported by evidence, which are contradicted or unsupported, and which records, approvals or Passports are missing or invalid.

T2 Inputs. Scope: a tenant, a date range or importer filter, up to 500 cases (D-28). Per case: the declaration facts (DECLARATION_DRAFT or recorded filed values as facts), canonical shipment facts (F01), Decisions and reason codes, Approvals and Overrides, Passports and their verification results (F06), open holds, unresolved F08 findings, document retention status.

T3 Sources and labels. All inputs are recorded evidence. The report states the weakest label it relies on and the share of cases with SYNTHETIC origin.

T4 Logic.
a) Item reconciliation. For every declared item (parties, value, currency, quantity, weights, classification, origin, incoterm, charges paid) find supporting evidence: SUPPORTED (an F01 canonical value or document fact agrees), CONTRADICTED (a verified fact differs), UNSUPPORTED (no evidence found), STALE (evidence older than the declaration's recorded time or superseded).
b) Record checks per case: required documents present for the workflow; Passport exists, is FINAL and verifies (using the standalone verifier of F06); every Override has a justification and, where the policy requires, a second reviewer; no unresolved F08 BLOCKING finding; no open HOLD or ESCALATE without a recorded resolution; documents referenced by hash are still retrievable or their erasure is recorded as an authorized erasure.
c) Gap list. Each gap has a kind, severity, case, citations and the action that would close it. Severity by kind: HIGH for CONTRADICTED_ITEM, PASSPORT_MISSING_OR_INVALID, OVERRIDE_INCOMPLETE, OPEN_HOLD, UNRESOLVED_FINDING; MEDIUM for UNSUPPORTED_ITEM, MISSING_DOCUMENT; LOW for RETENTION_GAP and STALE evidence. Kinds: UNSUPPORTED_ITEM, CONTRADICTED_ITEM, MISSING_DOCUMENT, PASSPORT_MISSING_OR_INVALID, OVERRIDE_INCOMPLETE, OPEN_HOLD, UNRESOLVED_FINDING, RETENTION_GAP.
d) Sampling. A reproducible sample (seeded from the scope hash; default 10 percent, at least 5 cases when available) is drawn for reviewer spot-checks, stratified by outcome.
e) Audit bundle. An index of Passports, replay bundles and reconciliation results, hash-bound, for hand-over to an auditor.

T5 Outputs. ReconciliationReport: scope and its hash, counts by status, gap kind and severity, gap list, sample, audit bundle index, labels and SYNTHETIC share. Status only.

T6 Status codes.
| Code | Meaning | Status |
|---|---|---|
| K03.REPORT.PROVIDED | all cases in scope reconciled | PROVIDED |
| K03.REPORT.PARTIAL | some cases could not be reconciled; listed | PROVIDED |
| K03.NEEDS_REVIEW.LEDGER_INTEGRITY | ledger verification failed for a case | NEEDS_REVIEW |
| K03.ABSTAIN.SCOPE_EMPTY | no case in scope | ABSTAINED |
| K03.ABSTAIN.SCOPE_TOO_LARGE | scope exceeds 500 cases | ABSTAINED |
| K03.ABSTAIN.DECLARATION_MISSING | no declaration facts for the case | ABSTAINED |

T7 Conditions. K03 reads and reports; it never edits a record to close a gap. K03 reads Passports and their verification results from F06 and nothing flows back: no K03 report enters a Passport.

T8 Evidence. Report record with scope hash, case ids, input commitments, output hash; the sample draw is reproducible from its seed.

T9 Budget. No model call; a scope of 500 cases within the Part 4 §4.14 budgets (batch budget stated in Part 6).

T10 Interfaces. API and command per 5.1.3. View: scope selector, gap list with filters by severity and kind, per-case drill-down to the evidence viewer, sample list, bundle download.

T11 Scenarios.
| ID | Description | Expected |
|---|---|---|
| K03-S01 | every declared item has agreeing evidence, Passports verify | zero gaps |
| K03-S02 | declared value differs from the invoice | CONTRADICTED_ITEM, HIGH |
| K03-S03 | declared origin has no document support | UNSUPPORTED_ITEM, MEDIUM |
| K03-S04 | Passport missing for a closed case | PASSPORT_MISSING_OR_INVALID |
| K03-S05 | Override without justification | OVERRIDE_INCOMPLETE |
| K03-S06 | ESCALATE left without a resolution | OPEN_HOLD |
| K03-S07 | scope of 501 cases | ABSTAIN, SCOPE_TOO_LARGE |
| K03-S08 | ledger gap in one case | NEEDS_REVIEW, LEDGER_INTEGRITY |
| K03-S09 | document erased under an authorized erasure | recorded erasure, not a gap |
| K03-S10 | same scope run twice | identical report and sample |

T12 Tests. Property: with no injected defects the gap list is empty; every injected defect in a generated corpus is found (recall 100 percent on SYNTHETIC defects); the sample is reproducible and stratified; scope selection never crosses a tenant boundary. Differential: reconciliation against a table-driven reference. Adversarial: Passport swapped between cases, duplicated case ids, huge scopes. Replay. Performance test for 500 cases.

T13 Responsible use. The report lists gaps; it never hides, reorders for effect or removes a gap, and the gap list cannot be filtered out of the bundle.

T14 Consumers. K04, K22, K19.

<<<P5-K04>>>
5.K04 VOLUNTARY DISCLOSURE NAVIGATOR
H: K04 · Voluntary Disclosure Navigator · v1.0.0 · class D+R · lead: Compliance · depends: K03 · output: ADV · covers PR-02, PR-03, PR-09, PR-17.

T1 Purpose. When an error in a past declaration is found, assemble the accurate evidence a person needs to correct it: what was declared, what the evidence supports, the difference, the money effect, the cases affected, and when the system first recorded the discrepancy.

T2 Inputs. A K03 gap (or a reviewer-flagged item) of kind CONTRADICTED_ITEM or UNSUPPORTED_ITEM; the evidence for the item; optional recorded outputs of L02 (landed cost) and F02 (classification) for the money effect (when absent the effect is "not computed" and no ordering dependency arises); the ledger.

T3 Sources and labels. Recorded evidence only. The pack states the weakest label it uses. Eligibility for any disclosure regime, deadlines and consequences are legal matters outside the system and are not assessed (V-08); the pack says so.

T4 Logic.
a) Corrected value. A corrected value is proposed only when the evidence supports exactly one value for the item (an F01-agreed or document-supported value). With zero or several supported values the pack lists the missing evidence and the competing values instead.
b) Difference and effect. Declared value, supported value, difference; money effect on duty and charges when L02 and F02 outputs are present, otherwise "not computed".
c) Discovery record. The ledger time at which the system first recorded the discrepancy (first K03 or F01 finding), stated as a fact with its record hash. It is the system's record time only; it says nothing about when any person knew.
d) Affected cases. All cases in the tenant sharing the root cause are found by deterministic grouping and all are included. The root-cause signature is the tuple (fact key, normalized supplier party, classification code at heading level, declared-versus-supported discrepancy direction); the signature fields are fixed in the pack and recorded. The pack cannot omit a case that the grouping finds. A reviewer may add cases, not remove them.
e) Statement of facts. Fixed templates produce neutral sentences, each tied to a citation; a sentence without a citation is dropped by the grounding check. Wording contains no admissions, no minimising language and no legal conclusions.
f) Export. A reviewer-approved local export (JSON and printable HTML). Submission to any authority is outside the system.

T5 Outputs. DisclosureEvidencePack: item, declared and supported values, difference, money effect, discovery record, affected cases with the signature used, statement of facts with citations, open questions, labels, hash.

T6 Status codes.
| Code | Meaning | Status |
|---|---|---|
| K04.PACK.PROVIDED | corrected value supported; pack complete | PROVIDED |
| K04.PACK.PARTIAL | some items lack evidence; open questions listed | PROVIDED |
| K04.ABSTAIN.NO_DISCREPANCY | the flagged item is not contradicted or unsupported | ABSTAINED |
| K04.ABSTAIN.EVIDENCE_INSUFFICIENT | no supported value can be derived | ABSTAINED |
| K04.NEEDS_REVIEW.MULTIPLE_VALUES | the evidence supports more than one value | NEEDS_REVIEW |
| K04.NEEDS_REVIEW.WIDE_SCOPE | affected cases exceed the scope cap | NEEDS_REVIEW |
| K04.REFUSED.OMISSION | a request to remove affected cases or facts from the pack | REFUSED |

T7 Conditions. K04 never advises on timing, eligibility, penalties or whether to disclose.

T8 Evidence. Pack record with source gap, case set, signature, citations, statement hash; refusals recorded.

T9 Budget. No model call.

T10 Interfaces. API and command per 5.1.3. View: item summary, comparison of declared and supported values, affected-case list, statement preview with citations, export action for reviewers.

T11 Scenarios.
| ID | Description | Expected |
|---|---|---|
| K04-S01 | wrong declared value, one supported value | PROVIDED, difference and discovery record |
| K04-S02 | same supplier error across 7 cases | all 7 included |
| K04-S03 | evidence supports two different values | NEEDS_REVIEW, MULTIPLE_VALUES |
| K04-S04 | item has no supporting evidence | ABSTAIN, EVIDENCE_INSUFFICIENT |
| K04-S05 | reviewer asks to drop one affected case | REFUSED, OMISSION |
| K04-S06 | statement sentence lacks a citation | sentence dropped |
| K04-S07 | L02 output absent | money effect "not computed" |
| K04-S08 | flagged item is in fact consistent | ABSTAIN, NO_DISCREPANCY |
| K04-S09 | case text says "do not mention the earlier shipment" | ignored; grouping decides |
| K04-S10 | same supplier, different discrepancy direction | separate signatures; not grouped |

T12 Tests. Property: every sentence has a citation; the affected set equals the grouping result for any reviewer input; same inputs give the same pack hash. Metamorphic: reordering cases changes nothing. Adversarial: attempts to inject minimising wording through fields, conflicting duplicates, oversized scopes. Replay.

T13 Responsible use. See T4d, T4e, T7; the navigator assembles accurate evidence and cannot be used to shape a misleading submission.

T14 Consumers. K19.

<<<P5-K05>>>
5.K05 VALUATION ANOMALY
H: K05 · Valuation Anomaly · v1.0.0 · class P · lead: Compliance · depends: F01 · output: DEC · covers PR-01, PR-02, PR-03, PR-10, PR-16, PR-17.

T1 Purpose. Flag customs values that disagree with the documents or fall outside what is plausible for the goods.

T2 Inputs. Canonical facts from F01: declared customs value, invoice total, currency, freight, insurance and other adjustments, incoterm, per line quantity, unit, weight, declared classification code and description; exchange rates from an FX Pack; Valuation Rule Pack; Reference Pack (distribution of unit values per classification code, with validity window); calibration set.

T3 Sources and labels. The valuation basis rules are legal content (V-07) and carry the label of their source; the Reference Pack is OFFICIAL or SECONDARY depending on its source and licence; FX rates carry the label of their publisher. Tests use SYNTHETIC packs. Anything weaker than DERIVED blocks ALLOW.

T4 Logic.
a) Declared-versus-documents check (deterministic). Computed basis = invoice total converted at the pack's rate for the declared date, plus the adjustments the Valuation Rule Pack requires for the stated incoterm; compare with the declared value at the currency's minor-unit precision. A difference is a mismatch. If the rule requires an adjustment (for example freight) whose fact is missing, the run abstains rather than assuming zero.
b) Unit value. Per line: value divided by the quantity in the reference basis (per kilogram, piece or litre), with units converted by the unit table and currency by the FX Pack.
c) Reference statistics. For the code at the pack's level: m_c = median of ln(unit value) and s_c = max(1.4826 × median absolute deviation of ln(unit value), s_min) with s_min = 0.05. If the sample is below 30, fall back to the parent level; the fallback is recorded; if still below 30, abstain. The Reference Pack must be valid at the evaluation time.
d) Nonconformity a = |ln(v) − m_c| / s_c.
e) Calibration (split conformal). q̂ is the ⌈(n+1)(1−α)⌉-th smallest nonconformity of the calibration draws (n ≥ 500, α = 0.10), drawn separately from the reference sample and bound to the Reference Pack version hash. Interval for ln(v): m_c ± q̂ × s_c.
f) Decision: declared unit value inside the interval and part a) passes: ALLOW (on real data only with the calibration basis of Part 5 §5.1.6a, otherwise ESCALATE with K05.REVIEW.UNVALIDATED_CALIBRATION); outside: ESCALATE with direction BELOW or ABOVE; part a) mismatch: HOLD. A per-code aggregate over all lines of the case with the same code is checked the same way, so splitting a shipment across lines does not hide a low average.
g) Visibility. The interval bounds are shown only to the reviewer and administrator roles; other roles see inside or outside the reference range and the direction, so the acceptable range cannot be learned and targeted.

T4a Probabilistic basis. Score and interval as in d) and e). Coverage claim, verbatim: "On the SYNTHETIC calibration distribution, and assuming exchangeability, a unit value generated by the reference process lies inside the interval with probability at least 90 percent (marginal). This statement does not apply to real prices and is not a valuation ruling." Diagnostics archived with each calibration: coverage on a held-out split and on a shifted set.

T5 Outputs. ValuationReport: part a) computation, per line and per code unit values, reference level used and sample size, interval (reviewer-only), direction, outcome, reasons, coverage claim and basis. Contribution: outcome.

T6 Reason codes.
| Code | Meaning | Outcome |
|---|---|---|
| K05.OK.WITHIN_RANGE | declared value agrees with documents and lies inside the reference interval | ALLOW |
| K05.VALUE.DECLARED_MISMATCH | declared value differs from the computed basis | HOLD |
| K05.ANOMALY.BELOW_RANGE | unit value below the interval | ESCALATE |
| K05.ANOMALY.ABOVE_RANGE | unit value above the interval | ESCALATE |
| K05.OBS.FALLBACK_LEVEL_USED | reference taken from a parent level | none (recorded) |
| K05.ABSTAIN.REFERENCE_INSUFFICIENT | sample too small at every level | ABSTAIN |
| K05.ABSTAIN.REFERENCE_EXPIRED | Reference Pack not valid at the evaluation time | ABSTAIN |
| K05.ABSTAIN.FX_UNAVAILABLE | no exchange rate for the currency and date | ABSTAIN |
| K05.ABSTAIN.UNIT_UNSUPPORTED | quantity unit cannot be converted | ABSTAIN |
| K05.ABSTAIN.VALUATION_RULE_UNAVAILABLE | no valuation rule for the incoterm | ABSTAIN |
| K05.ABSTAIN.ADJUSTMENT_MISSING | a required adjustment fact is missing | ABSTAIN |
| K05.ABSTAIN.CALIBRATION_STALE | calibration not bound to this Reference Pack version | ABSTAIN |
| K05.ABSTAIN.UPSTREAM | F01 canonical facts missing | ABSTAIN |
| K05.ABSTAIN.LABEL_INSUFFICIENT | a pack weaker than the ALLOW condition | ABSTAIN |
| K05.REVIEW.UNVALIDATED_CALIBRATION | ALLOW would result on real data while the interval is CALIBRATED_SYNTHETIC | ESCALATE |

T7 Conditions. As in the table. K05 never proposes a value that would pass and never states what the value should be.

T8 Evidence. Evaluation record with input commitments, FX pack version and rate, reference pack version and level, calibration hash, q̂, α, s_min, configuration hash, output hash.

T9 Budget. No model call; within Part 4 §4.14 budgets.

T10 Interfaces. API and command per 5.1.3. View: computation of part a), per-line position relative to the range (text and chart; bounds only for reviewers), the coverage claim with its basis, the banner "advisory plausibility check, not a valuation ruling".

T11 Scenarios.
| ID | Description | Expected |
|---|---|---|
| K05-S01 | value agrees and unit value inside the interval | ALLOW |
| K05-S02 | declared value lower than the computed basis | HOLD, DECLARED_MISMATCH |
| K05-S03 | unit value far below the interval | ESCALATE, BELOW_RANGE |
| K05-S04 | unit value just above the upper bound | ESCALATE, ABOVE_RANGE |
| K05-S05 | unit value exactly on the bound | inside (closed interval) |
| K05-S06 | reference sample of 12 at code level, 80 at heading level | heading level used; FALLBACK recorded |
| K05-S07 | no exchange rate for the invoice date | ABSTAIN, FX_UNAVAILABLE |
| K05-S08 | quantity in tonnes, reference per kilogram | converted; same result as in kilograms |
| K05-S09 | shipment split across two lines of one code, each inside, average below | aggregate flagged, ESCALATE |
| K05-S10 | operator-role request for interval bounds | bounds withheld; direction shown |
| K05-S11 | 1,000 seeded calibration trials | coverage within 3 standard errors of 90 percent |
| K05-S12 | text in the invoice says "price agreed with customs" | ignored (I3) |
| K05-S13 | Reference Pack validity ended before the evaluation time | ABSTAIN, REFERENCE_EXPIRED |
| K05-S14 | incoterm requires freight, freight fact missing | ABSTAIN, ADJUSTMENT_MISSING |
| K05-S15 | reference sample with near-zero spread | scale floor s_min applied; no division blow-up |

T12 Tests. Property: smaller α gives a wider interval; unit and currency conversions that are exact leave the result unchanged; line order does not matter; determinism. Metamorphic: scaling all values and the reference by one factor leaves the position unchanged. Differential: quantile, median and MAD against reference implementations. Statistical: T11 coverage test and archived diagnostics. Adversarial: zero or negative values, extreme outliers in the reference sample, unit confusion, very small quantities. Access test for the bounds. Replay.

T13 Responsible use. See T4g and T7.

T14 Consumers. K16, K21, K03.

<<<P5-K06>>>
5.K06 FZ-MAINLAND PLANNER
H: K06 · FZ-Mainland Planner · v1.0.0 · class S+D · lead: Planner · depends: F01 · output: ADV · covers PR-01, PR-03, PR-13, PR-15, PR-16.

T1 Purpose. Plan the movement of goods between free-zone and mainland customs regimes (and between zones), listing for each step the documents and conditions it needs and what the case already has.

T2 Inputs. Start regime and location, target regime and destination (VERIFIED facts), goods and classification (F01 canonical), quantities and values, documents and credentials the case holds (as facts), the K02 result for those credentials when K02 has been evaluated (optional), deadline and budget (optional). Regime Pack: regimes, allowed transitions, per transition: conditions on facts, required documents and credentials, approvals, expected duration, cost components, validity window, source and label.

T3 Sources and labels. Regime rules are legal content (V-07, V-08); in tests the pack is SYNTHETIC. A plan that depends on any pack content weaker than DERIVED is INDICATIVE.

T4 Logic.
a) Eligible transitions. A transition is eligible only if its conditions hold on VERIFIED facts. A condition on a fact that is not VERIFIED makes the transition ineligible and is reported as the blocker.
b) Search. Feasible sequences of transitions from start to target under the deadline and budget constraints, ranked lexicographically by cost, then time, then step count (three sequential CP-SAT stages, one worker, fixed seed, each stage fixing the optimum of the previous one); up to 3 alternatives by no-good cuts. The 20 s time limit is shared by all stages of the run.
c) Checklists. For each step: documents and credentials needed, each marked HAVE (VERIFIED in the case) or MISSING; for credentials, K02's validity result (VALID, INVALID, NOT_EVALUATED) is shown beside the mark and K06 never judges validity itself.
d) Verification. An independent exact verifier recomputes preconditions, durations and costs for every plan.
e) No plan. The report lists the minimal set of unmet conditions that blocks every sequence.
f) A plan whose only advantage rests on a condition fact that is not VERIFIED is never offered.

T5 Outputs. RegimePlan: up to 3 sequences with steps, checklists, durations, costs, labels and optimality status, or the blocking conditions. Status only.

T6 Status codes.
| Code | Meaning | Status |
|---|---|---|
| K06.PLAN.PROVIDED | plans found | PROVIDED |
| K06.PLAN.INDICATIVE | pack content weaker than DERIVED | PROVIDED |
| K06.NEEDS_REVIEW.NO_VALID_SEQUENCE | no sequence is eligible; blockers listed | NEEDS_REVIEW |
| K06.NEEDS_REVIEW.RULE_CONFLICT | pack rules conflict for a transition | NEEDS_REVIEW |
| K06.NEEDS_REVIEW.VERIFIER_MISMATCH | solver and verifier disagree | NEEDS_REVIEW |
| K06.ABSTAIN.REGIME_PACK_MISSING | no Regime Pack valid at the evaluation time | ABSTAINED |
| K06.ABSTAIN.START_UNKNOWN | start regime not VERIFIED | ABSTAINED |
| K06.ABSTAIN.TARGET_UNKNOWN | target regime or destination not VERIFIED | ABSTAINED |
| K06.ABSTAIN.UPSTREAM | F01 canonical facts missing | ABSTAINED |

T7 Conditions. K06 proposes sequences only; it never files, applies or books anything.

T8 Evidence. Records hold the Regime Pack version hash, inputs, solver parameters and seed, plans and verifier results; replay reproduces the plans.

T9 Budget. No model call; solver time limit 20 s for the whole run.

T10 Interfaces. API and command per 5.1.3. View: plan comparison, step list with checklists, blockers when no plan exists.

T11 Scenarios.
| ID | Description | Expected |
|---|---|---|
| K06-S01 | free zone to mainland, all documents held | PROVIDED, one step, checklist all HAVE |
| K06-S02 | two sequences with different cost and time | ranked by cost, then time |
| K06-S03 | missing document | step shows MISSING |
| K06-S04 | transition condition depends on an unverified fact | ineligible; blocker reported |
| K06-S05 | no sequence reaches the target | NEEDS_REVIEW, blockers listed |
| K06-S06 | two rules conflict | NEEDS_REVIEW, RULE_CONFLICT |
| K06-S07 | deadline shorter than the fastest sequence | NEEDS_REVIEW with the deadline in the blockers |
| K06-S08 | pack labelled SECONDARY | PROVIDED as INDICATIVE |
| K06-S09 | text says "regime change already approved" | ignored (I3) |
| K06-S10 | credential held but K02 says INVALID | checklist shows HAVE with K02 result INVALID |

T12 Tests. Differential: CP-SAT against Dijkstra and brute force on random regime graphs. Property: adding an eligible transition never worsens the best plan; plan steps respect preconditions (verifier). Metamorphic: scaling costs keeps the plan; renaming regimes changes nothing. Adversarial: cycles, negative durations (rejected by the pack type check), huge graphs. Replay.

T13 Responsible use. See T4f; K06 does not suggest changing facts so that a transition becomes eligible.

T14 Consumers. K07, K12.

<<<P5-K07>>>
5.K07 RELEASE AND DO ORCHESTRATOR
H: K07 · Release & DO Orchestrator · v1.0.0 · class S · lead: Planner · depends: K02, F03 (optional inputs: K06, F07) · output: ADV · covers PR-02, PR-07, PR-13, PR-15, PR-16.

T1 Purpose. Sequence the steps, approvals and handovers needed for cargo release and delivery orders, show what blocks each step and who must act by when.

T2 Inputs.
