f) Claims boundary text, fixed: "This certificate states that the recorded evaluation of the listed checks was intact and reproducible at issue. It is not a statement of legal compliance, an official ruling, or an approval by any authority." For data_origin SYNTHETIC the certificate is stamped SYNTHETIC and supports no real-world claim (D-26).
g) Supersession. A reopened case supersedes its certificate: the Kernel publishes the superseded certificate id on a signed status list (hash-chained, checkpointed). A verifier that is given the list reports SUPERSEDED; without the list the certificate states that it cannot know.
h) Offline verifier: a standalone module (no Kernel dependency; same signature library as F06, V-22). It checks: schema; payload hash; signature and key validity at issue time; Passport reference and, when the Passport is supplied, its F06 verification; every statement's inclusion proof; consistency between each statement and the record it cites (capability, outcome, reason codes, versions); coverage counts; status list when supplied. Exit codes as F06.

T5 Outputs. Certificate file (payload and detached signature), verification report, human-readable view generated from the payload. Status only.

T6 Status codes.
| Code | Meaning | Status |
|---|---|---|
| K18.CERT.ISSUED | certificate issued | PROVIDED |
| K18.REFUSED.PASSPORT_DRAFT | the Passport is DRAFT | REFUSED |
| K18.REFUSED.UNRESOLVED_BLOCKER | a HOLD or ESCALATE has no recorded Approval | REFUSED |
| K18.ABSTAIN.INCOMPLETE_EVALUATIONS | a configured capability has no non-stale evaluation | ABSTAINED |
| K18.ABSTAIN.KEY_UNAVAILABLE | no signing key available | ABSTAINED |
| K18.NEEDS_REVIEW.LEDGER_INTEGRITY | ledger verification failed | NEEDS_REVIEW |
Verifier result codes: CERT.OK; CERT.FAIL.SCHEMA, .PAYLOAD_HASH, .SIGNATURE, .KEY_NOT_VALID_AT_TIME, .PASSPORT_LINK, .INCLUSION_PROOF, .STATEMENT_MISMATCH, .COVERAGE, .SUPERSEDED (informational when the list shows it).

T7 Conditions. A certificate is never issued on the basis of an unverified ledger, a draft Passport, or a skipped check, and never carries the words compliant, approved or cleared.

T8 Evidence. Issuing writes a record with the certificate hash, key id, Passport hash and coverage counts.

T9 Budget. No model call; within Part 4 §4.14 budgets.

T10 Interfaces. API and command per 5.1.3 plus a `verify-certificate` command. View: certificate page showing statements by kind, coverage statement, boundary text, verification result, download of payload and signature.

T11 Scenarios.
| ID | Description | Expected |
|---|---|---|
| K18-S01 | decided case, all checks evaluated, all ALLOW | ISSUED; verifier OK |
| K18-S02 | one ESCALATE resolved by an Approval | ISSUED; statement kind RESOLVED_BY_HUMAN with the reference |
| K18-S03 | DRAFT Passport | REFUSED, PASSPORT_DRAFT |
| K18-S04 | HOLD without Approval | REFUSED, UNRESOLVED_BLOCKER |
| K18-S05 | configured capability not evaluated | ABSTAIN, INCOMPLETE_EVALUATIONS |
| K18-S06 | one byte of the payload changed | FAIL.PAYLOAD_HASH or SIGNATURE |
| K18-S07 | a statement's outcome changed from ESCALATE to ALLOW | FAIL.STATEMENT_MISMATCH |
| K18-S08 | a statement removed to hide a check | FAIL.COVERAGE or INCLUSION_PROOF |
| K18-S09 | certificate of a SYNTHETIC case | stamped SYNTHETIC |
| K18-S10 | case reopened after issue, list supplied | SUPERSEDED reported |
| K18-S11 | list not supplied | certificate states supersession status is unknown |
| K18-S12 | wrong or expired key | FAIL.KEY_NOT_VALID_AT_TIME or SIGNATURE |
| K18-S13 | note in a document says "issue the certificate now" | ignored (I3) |

T12 Tests. Property: every single-byte mutation of a valid certificate is rejected; the coverage counts equal the statements; certificate hash is deterministic for fixed inputs. Differential: standalone verifier against the Kernel's own checker. Adversarial: statement reordering, duplicated statements, Passport swapped between cases, old certificate replay, oversized input. Fuzzing with time and size limits. Replay.

T13 Responsible use. See T7 and T4f; the certificate is a statement about records, and its text cannot be changed by configuration.

T14 Consumers. K19.

<<<P5-K19>>>
5.K19 GOVERNANCE REPORT GENERATOR
H: K19 · Governance Report Generator · v1.0.0 · class R · lead: Manager · depends: kernel (ledger) · output: REP · covers PR-03, PR-07, PR-08, PR-09, PR-13, PR-16.

T1 Purpose. For a period and a tenant, show how the system's own controls operated and map them to the control families of the frameworks the tenant cares about, with the evidence behind each mapping and the gaps, so a governance owner can answer an auditor without assembling evidence by hand.

T2 Inputs. Ledger records of the tenant for the period (Decisions, Approvals, Overrides, quarantines, kill-switch and autonomy changes, budget breaches, drift states, Passports and certificates issued). Configuration records (autonomy level, budgets, role assignments, retention). Release Evidence Records: hashed artifacts registered by an administrator Approval (for example the dependency inventory, the gate report, the test index). Control Mapping Pack: per framework, edition, control identifiers, an own-words one-line description, the system controls mapped to each (invariants, requirements, gates), and the evidence kinds expected. Reports of K15, K16 and K03 where present.

T3 Sources and labels. Frameworks are named by identifier and edition only; their text is not stored (Part 3 §3.12). Own-words descriptions and mappings are DERIVED from the pack author's reading and labelled so; they are not certifications or statements by the framework owner. Editions are unverified until checked (V-10).

T4 Logic.
a) Control evidence. For each mapped system control, the evidence kinds are LEDGER_STAT (computed from the ledger), CONFIGURATION (read from the configuration hash and records), RELEASE_ARTIFACT (hash of a registered artifact), REPORT (a K03, K15 or K16 report). Evidence status: EVIDENCED (every expected kind present), PARTIAL, NO_EVIDENCE, NOT_APPLICABLE (pack-declared for the deployment).
b) Statistics (period = one calendar month, or a range of months): Decisions by outcome and capability; abstention and escalation rates; Approvals and Overrides counts, justification completeness and second-reviewer completeness; time from ESCALATE to resolution; quarantines and kill-switch events with reasons; model calls by processing region and calls refused by REGION_POLICY (the residency report); budget breaches; drift states; Passports and certificates issued; refused requests by reason code.
c) Exceptions list: every Override, quarantine, kill-switch use, drift event and refusal in the period, each with its record reference. The list cannot be filtered out of the exported report.
d) Small-cell suppression. In any breakdown shown beyond the tenant's administrators, a count below 5 is shown as "fewer than 5". A platform-wide report across tenants contains aggregates only, with the same suppression, and never a tenant identifier.
e) Gaps. Controls with PARTIAL or NO_EVIDENCE are listed first, with what would complete them.
f) Hash binding. The report lists the hashes of the records and artifacts it summarizes, so a reader can recompute the statistics.
g) Wording. Statements use fixed templates: "evidence present", "evidence partial", "no evidence recorded"; the report never says that a framework is satisfied, certified or compliant.

T5 Outputs. GovernanceReport: period, tenant scope, control map with evidence status per control, statistics, exceptions list, gaps, hashes, labels, export (JSON and printable HTML). Status only.

T6 Status codes.
| Code | Meaning | Status |
|---|---|---|
| K19.REPORT.PROVIDED | all mapped controls have EVIDENCED or NOT_APPLICABLE status | PROVIDED |
| K19.REPORT.PARTIAL | some controls PARTIAL or NO_EVIDENCE; gaps listed | PROVIDED |
| K19.NEEDS_REVIEW.LEDGER_INTEGRITY | ledger verification failed for part of the period | NEEDS_REVIEW |
| K19.ABSTAIN.MAPPING_PACK_MISSING | no Control Mapping Pack valid at the evaluation time | ABSTAINED |
| K19.ABSTAIN.PERIOD_EMPTY | no records in the period | ABSTAINED |

T7 Conditions. K19 reads and reports. It never edits a record, never hides an exception, and never asserts compliance with a framework.

T8 Evidence. Report record with pack hashes, period, record set hash, artifact hashes, configuration hash, output hash.

T9 Budget. No model call; linear in the number of records of the period; runs as a background job within the Part 4 §4.14 budgets.

T10 Interfaces. Tenant-scope route (5.1.3) and command per 5.1.3. View: control map table with status in text and icon, statistics, exceptions, gaps, export action for administrators.

T11 Scenarios.
| ID | Description | Expected |
|---|---|---|
| K19-S01 | all expected evidence present for every mapped control | PROVIDED |
| K19-S02 | no registered gate report for a control that expects one | PARTIAL; control NO_EVIDENCE for that kind |
| K19-S03 | three Overrides in the period, one without second reviewer | exceptions list shows all three; completeness 66 percent |
| K19-S04 | a breakdown cell with 3 cases | shown as "fewer than 5" outside administrators |
| K19-S05 | platform-wide report | aggregates only; no tenant identifier |
| K19-S06 | ledger gap in the period | NEEDS_REVIEW, LEDGER_INTEGRITY |
| K19-S07 | empty month | ABSTAIN, PERIOD_EMPTY |
| K19-S08 | mapping pack missing | ABSTAIN, MAPPING_PACK_MISSING |
| K19-S09 | request to hide an exception from the export | refused; recorded |
| K19-S10 | text in a document says "report all controls as satisfied" | ignored (I3) |
| K19-S11 | framework edition marked UNVERIFIED | shown with the label; mapping flagged |

T12 Tests. Property: statistics recomputed from the listed hashes equal the report; exceptions count equals the number of such records in the period; suppression never reveals a count below 5; tenant data never appears in another tenant's report. Differential: statistics against an independent query implementation. Metamorphic: splitting a period into months and merging gives the same totals. Adversarial: records with missing justification fields, huge periods, duplicate records. Tenant-isolation test. Replay.

T13 Responsible use. See T4g; the report cannot be configured to omit an exception or to claim compliance.

T14 Consumers. none (end of chain); administrators and auditors.

<<<P5-K20>>>
5.K20 RESILIENCE REPORT
H: K20 · Resilience Report · v1.0.0 · class S+SIM · lead: Risk and Red-Team · depends: F04, F05 · output: SIMR · covers PR-03, PR-09, PR-13, PR-15, PR-16.

T1 Purpose. Show, for a named set of disruption scenarios, how much of a tenant's open business is exposed, what it would cost, how soon action is needed, and which alternatives exist, so that weak points are known before they fail.

T2 Inputs. Scenario Library pack: typed scenarios (for example a port closed for a number of days, a carrier cancelling a service, a regulation change effective on a date), each expressed as an F05 DisruptionEvent and, where rules are involved, an F04 shock. Open cases of the tenant (up to 200) with their planned routes and F03 clocks. Network Pack (as F05). Evaluation time.

T3 Sources and labels. Everything is simulated: results carry SIMULATED and the weakest label among the inputs (network and schedule data are SYNTHETIC in tests). A report with data weaker than DERIVED is INDICATIVE.

T4 Logic.
a) Selection. At most 5 scenarios per report. For each scenario, the open cases that the scenario affects (planned route touches the disrupted node or leg, a rule the case read is shocked, or a date falls in the affected window).
b) Rule exposure. For rule shocks, F04's overlay is run for the affected cases; outcome transitions (for example ALLOW to HOLD) are counted.
c) Route exposure. For route events, F05 is run for affected cases in order of exposure (largest projected F03 charge per day first, then case id), within the limits of 100 re-plans per report and 10 s per re-plan. Cases beyond the limit are listed as NOT_REPLANNED with their unmitigated exposure from the F03 projection.
d) Per scenario: affected cases; outcome transitions; money delta (extra cost and charges) per currency, unmitigated and with the best verified plan; earliest time at which action is needed; cases with no feasible plan within their deadline or budget; the relaxation set reported by F05 for the first five of them.
e) Concentration. From the planned routes, the share of open cases and of projected value that depend on each node or leg, and a list of single points of failure: nodes or legs whose loss leaves some case without a feasible plan.
f) Indicators for the tenant: share of affected cases with a feasible alternative; worst-case money delta per scenario; the node with the highest dependence. Indicators are descriptive; no score is invented.
g) Guards. Scenarios cannot override screening or red-flag facts (F04 guard); F05 plans keep every screening and credential requirement as a constraint; a write attempt stops the run.
h) Output kind SIMR: the report never becomes a Decision and enters a Passport only as a labelled annex.

T5 Outputs. ResilienceReport: scenario list, per-scenario exposure table, concentration table, single points of failure, NOT_REPLANNED list, indicators, labels, SIMULATED marker, parameters used. Status only.

T6 Status codes.
| Code | Meaning | Status |
|---|---|---|
| K20.REPORT.PROVIDED | all affected cases were evaluated | PROVIDED |
| K20.REPORT.PARTIAL | some affected cases were not re-planned because of the limits | PROVIDED |
| K20.REPORT.INDICATIVE | input data weaker than DERIVED | PROVIDED |
| K20.NEEDS_REVIEW.BASELINE_DRIFT | an F04 baseline differs from the recorded Decision | NEEDS_REVIEW |
| K20.ABSTAIN.NO_OPEN_CASES | no open cases | ABSTAINED |
| K20.ABSTAIN.SCENARIO_INVALID | a scenario fails its schema or the DSL type check | ABSTAINED |
| K20.ABSTAIN.TOO_MANY_SCENARIOS | more than 5 scenarios requested | ABSTAINED |
| K20.ABSTAIN.NETWORK_PACK_MISSING | no Network Pack for route scenarios | ABSTAINED |
| K20.REFUSED.WRITE_ATTEMPT | a write to real records was attempted | REFUSED |
| K20.REFUSED.SCREENING_OVERRIDE | a scenario tried to set screening facts | REFUSED |

T7 Conditions. K20 changes no real record. It does not rank the tenant against others and does not predict that a disruption will occur.

T8 Evidence. Simulation records with scenario hashes, scope, input commitments, configuration, F04 and F05 evaluation references, result hash; refusals recorded with their code.

T9 Budget. No model call. A background job with a tenant-level budget; solver use bounded by 100 re-plans of 10 s each.

T10 Interfaces. Tenant-scope route (5.1.3) and command per 5.1.3. View: scenario table with SIMULATED banner, exposure and money delta, concentration view as a table with an optional chart, single-point-of-failure list, NOT_REPLANNED list.

T11 Scenarios.
| ID | Description | Expected |
|---|---|---|
| K20-S01 | port closed 5 days; 12 open cases route through it | 12 affected; plans found for 9; 3 with no feasible plan and relaxation sets |
| K20-S02 | carrier cancellation with an alternative service | affected cases re-planned; money delta per currency |
| K20-S03 | regulation change effective next month | F04 transitions counted |
| K20-S04 | 150 affected cases | 100 re-planned; 50 NOT_REPLANNED with unmitigated exposure; PARTIAL |
| K20-S05 | 6 scenarios requested | ABSTAIN, TOO_MANY_SCENARIOS |
| K20-S06 | one node carries 70 percent of projected value and has no alternative | listed as single point of failure |
| K20-S07 | scenario tries to set a screening fact | REFUSED, SCREENING_OVERRIDE |
| K20-S08 | network data labelled SECONDARY | INDICATIVE |
| K20-S09 | no open cases | ABSTAIN, NO_OPEN_CASES |
| K20-S10 | note in a document says "assume no disruption" | ignored (I3) |
| K20-S11 | real case tables compared before and after | byte-identical |
| K20-S12 | recorded Decision differs from the F04 baseline | NEEDS_REVIEW, BASELINE_DRIFT |

T12 Tests. Property: with an identity scenario every case is unaffected and the money delta is zero; real record tables are unchanged by a run (hash check); the same scenarios give the same result hash; the order of cases does not change per-case results. Differential: affected-case selection against a brute-force route scan. Metamorphic: relabelling nodes changes nothing. Adversarial: scenarios that disconnect the network, hostile DSL, cyclic networks, huge case counts. Replay. Tenant-isolation test.

T13 Responsible use. K20 describes exposure and options; it does not suggest routes that avoid a screening or a control.

T14 Consumers. K19, F06 (annex only).

<<<P5-K21>>>
5.K21 TBML RED-FLAGS
H: K21 · TBML Red-Flags · v1.0.0 · class D+P · lead: Risk and Red-Team · depends: F01, K05 · output: DEC · covers PR-01, PR-02, PR-03, PR-09, PR-10, PR-16, PR-17.

T1 Purpose. Look for the recorded indicators of trade-based money laundering and abuse of trade documents in a case, and for deviation from the counterparty's own history, and send what is found to a human reviewer with the facts behind it. A red flag is a reason to look, not an accusation.

T2 Inputs. F01 canonical facts; K05's valuation result for the case; VERIFIED facts: parties, bank countries and payer where present, payment terms, routes and route points, dates, document revision counts and fact supersessions; optional ownership facts. Indicator Pack (indicators with weights, parameters and thresholds). High-Risk Jurisdiction List Pack (a registered list, loaded like K01's lists, with publication date and label). Network Pack distances (optional). The counterparty's history within the same tenant (earlier cases, with their feature values). Calibration set. Evaluation time.

T3 Sources and labels. Indicator definitions come from public typologies chosen by the pack author and are labelled by source (V-08); lists carry the label of their source. In tests all packs are SYNTHETIC. A pack or list weaker than DERIVED blocks ALLOW. History is tenant data; it never crosses tenants.

T4 Logic. Mode: PROTECTIVE for counterparty identity (look-alike characters folded).
a) Indicators, each evaluated only when its facts are VERIFIED; those that cannot be evaluated are listed in the coverage record, never counted as clean:
 - IND-01 value anomaly: K05 reports an anomaly or a declared-value mismatch (MEDIUM).
 - IND-02 generic goods description: the description matches the pack's list of generic terms and the classification does not narrow it (MEDIUM).
 - IND-03 bank country differs from the countries of seller and buyer without a stated reason fact (MEDIUM).
 - IND-04 third-party payer: the payer differs from the buyer and no VERIFIED relationship fact exists (HIGH).
 - IND-05 circuitous route: total route length over direct length above the pack's factor, or transit through a listed high-risk jurisdiction off the direct path (MEDIUM).
 - IND-06 high-risk jurisdiction involved in a party, bank or route point (MEDIUM).
 - IND-07 repeated amendment: the number of superseded facts or document revisions for value, quantity or parties reaches the pack's count (MEDIUM).
 - IND-08 atypical payment terms: full advance payment with a counterparty that has fewer than 20 prior cases (MEDIUM).
 - IND-09 impossible timing: arrival before shipment, or a document dated before the event it describes (HIGH).
 - IND-10 round amounts: every line amount is a multiple of the pack's round unit (LOW).
b) Deterministic outcome. Any HIGH indicator, or at least two MEDIUM indicators: ESCALATE. LOW indicators and a single MEDIUM indicator are observations.
c) Counterparty profile (probabilistic part). For a counterparty with at least 20 prior cases in the tenant, features of the case (unit-value ratio to the reference from K05, route circuity ratio, payment-term category, revision count) are compared with the counterparty's own history by robust z-scores (median and scaled median absolute deviation, with a floor); nonconformity = the largest absolute robust z-score across the features. The flag threshold q̂ is the ⌈(n+1)(1−α)⌉-th smallest nonconformity of the calibration draws (n ≥ 500, α = 0.10), calibrated on SYNTHETIC histories and bound to the Indicator Pack version. A case with nonconformity above q̂ is a PROFILE_DEVIATION.
d) Combination. PROFILE_DEVIATION together with at least one indicator of any weight: ESCALATE. PROFILE_DEVIATION alone is an observation shown to reviewers. A counterparty with fewer than 20 prior cases has no profile; this is stated, not treated as normal.
e) Outcome: no indicator and no deviation, all lists fresh and sufficiently labelled: ALLOW (on real data only with the calibration basis of Part 5 §5.1.6a, otherwise ESCALATE with K21.REVIEW.UNVALIDATED_CALIBRATION); otherwise as above; missing facts or packs: ABSTAIN.
f) Visibility. The indicator details, thresholds, weights and profile numbers are visible to reviewer and administrator roles only. Other roles see a generic "compliance review required" for the ESCALATE, with no indicator or source named, so the product cannot be used to alert a counterparty. Each view of the details is recorded on the ledger.
g) The result is never labelled as a finding of wrongdoing; reporting decisions and any duty to report belong to people and are outside the system (V-08).

T4a Probabilistic basis. Score as in c); calibration as in c); set-size rule not applicable (a threshold, not a set). Coverage claim, verbatim: "On the SYNTHETIC calibration distribution, and assuming exchangeability between a counterparty's earlier cases and the new case, a case that follows the counterparty's usual pattern is flagged with probability at most 10 percent (marginal). This statement says nothing about the detection of money laundering and does not apply to real histories." Diagnostics archived with each calibration: false-flag rate on a held-out split and on a shifted set.

T5 Outputs. RedFlagReport: coverage record (indicators evaluated, not evaluated and why; lists and versions; profile available or not), indicators raised with cited facts (reviewer-only details), profile deviation (reviewer-only numbers), outcome and reason codes, coverage claim, template-rendered explanation. Contribution: outcome.

T6 Reason codes.
| Code | Meaning | Outcome |
|---|---|---|
| K21.OK.NO_INDICATORS | no indicator or deviation; coverage recorded | ALLOW |
| K21.REVIEW.HIGH_INDICATOR | at least one HIGH indicator | ESCALATE |
| K21.REVIEW.COMBINED_INDICATORS | at least two MEDIUM indicators | ESCALATE |
| K21.REVIEW.PROFILE_WITH_INDICATOR | profile deviation plus an indicator | ESCALATE |
| K21.OBS.SINGLE_INDICATOR | one MEDIUM or LOW indicator | none |
| K21.OBS.PROFILE_DEVIATION_ALONE | profile deviation without an indicator | none |
| K21.OBS.PROFILE_UNAVAILABLE | fewer than 20 prior cases | none (coverage) |
| K21.OBS.INDICATOR_NOT_EVALUATED | an indicator lacked verified facts | none (coverage) |
| K21.ABSTAIN.PACK_UNAVAILABLE | no Indicator Pack valid at the evaluation time | ABSTAIN |
| K21.ABSTAIN.LIST_UNAVAILABLE | the jurisdiction list is not loaded | ABSTAIN |
| K21.ABSTAIN.LIST_STALE | the list is older than the freshness limit | ABSTAIN |
| K21.ABSTAIN.CALIBRATION_STALE | calibration not bound to this pack version | ABSTAIN |
| K21.ABSTAIN.FACT_NOT_VERIFIED | a needed fact is PROPOSED or UNKNOWN | ABSTAIN |
| K21.ABSTAIN.UPSTREAM | F01 canonical facts or K05 result missing | ABSTAIN |
| K21.ABSTAIN.LABEL_INSUFFICIENT | a pack or list weaker than the ALLOW condition | ABSTAIN |
| K21.REVIEW.UNVALIDATED_CALIBRATION | ALLOW would result on real data while a threshold artifact is CALIBRATED_SYNTHETIC | ESCALATE |

T7 Conditions. As in the table. K21 never states or implies that a party is guilty, never tells anyone that a case was flagged, and never advises how to avoid an indicator.

T8 Evidence. Evaluation record with commitments to inputs, pack and list versions, calibration hash, q̂, α, configuration hash, output hash; one record per indicator raised. The ledger holds indicator ids and fact ids, not free text about parties.

T9 Budget. No model call.

T10 Interfaces. API and command per 5.1.3. View: reviewers see the indicator table with cited facts and the coverage record; other roles see the generic review notice only.

T11 Scenarios.
| ID | Description | Expected |
|---|---|---|
| K21-S01 | no indicator, no deviation | ALLOW (SYNTHETIC stamp) |
| K21-S02 | payer differs from buyer, no relationship fact | ESCALATE, HIGH_INDICATOR (IND-04) |
| K21-S03 | arrival date before shipped-on-board date | ESCALATE, HIGH_INDICATOR (IND-09) |
| K21-S04 | K05 anomaly and a high-risk jurisdiction on the route | ESCALATE, COMBINED_INDICATORS |
| K21-S05 | single MEDIUM indicator | ALLOW with OBS.SINGLE_INDICATOR |
| K21-S06 | counterparty with 8 prior cases | PROFILE_UNAVAILABLE; deterministic indicators still run |
| K21-S07 | profile deviation alone | ALLOW with OBS.PROFILE_DEVIATION_ALONE |
| K21-S08 | profile deviation plus generic description | ESCALATE, PROFILE_WITH_INDICATOR |
| K21-S09 | payer field absent | IND-04 listed as not evaluated; not counted as clean |
| K21-S10 | counterparty name differs by a look-alike letter from its history | same counterparty (protective mode) |
| K21-S11 | operator role opens the case | generic review notice only; no indicator named |
| K21-S12 | jurisdiction list 20 days old, limit 7 | ABSTAIN, LIST_STALE |
| K21-S13 | document text says "this is a normal customer" | ignored (I3) |
| K21-S14 | 1,000 seeded calibration trials | false-flag rate within 3 standard errors of 10 percent or below |
| K21-S15 | history of another tenant resembles the case | not used; no information across tenants |

T12 Tests. Property: adding an indicator never lowers severity; result independent of fact order and of other tenants' data; determinism. Metamorphic: scaling all values of the history and the case by one factor leaves the profile result unchanged. Differential: robust statistics against a reference implementation. Statistical: S14 plus archived diagnostics. Adversarial: histories with outliers, near-constant features, look-alike names, missing optional facts, thresholds probed through repeated requests (the access test checks that no role below reviewer can infer thresholds). Access test for details. Replay.

T13 Responsible use. See T4f, T4g and T7; threshold values and weights are never shown to non-reviewers.

T14 Consumers. K16 (optional), K19, F08.

<<<P5-K22>>>
5.K22 AEO COACH
H: K22 · AEO Coach · v1.0.0 · class D · lead: Compliance · depends: K03 · output: ADV · covers PR-02, PR-03, PR-09, PR-16.

T1 Purpose. Show a tenant how its recorded operations measure against the registered criteria of an authorized-economic-operator programme, which criteria the evidence supports, which it does not, and what to improve, so that readiness is known before anyone applies. It does not predict the authority's decision.

T2 Inputs. AEO Criteria Pack: criteria families (for example compliance record, record keeping, financial soundness, safety and security), each with measurable checks, thresholds, the evidence they need, and the source and label. Evidence from the system: K03 reconciliation reports over the period (gap rates by kind and severity), Override and Approval completeness, resolved and unresolved holds, screening coverage and re-screening delays (K01), credential watchlist (K02), retention and erasure records, budget and drift states where relevant. Attestations: statements entered by the tenant for criteria the system cannot observe (each with author, date and attached document hashes), clearly marked SELF_DECLARED. Period: the last 12 months, compared with the previous 12.

T3 Sources and labels. Criteria are legal and programme content (V-07, V-08), labelled by source; in tests SYNTHETIC. Measures computed from the ledger are DERIVED; attestations are SELF_DECLARED and never raise a criterion above PARTIAL by themselves.

T4 Logic.
a) Scope. All the tenant's cases in the period. No filter exists that excludes cases; the report shows the number of cases covered and, separately, the number with data_origin SYNTHETIC.
b) Measures. Each check computes a value from the evidence (for example the share of cases with a HIGH reconciliation gap, the share of Overrides with complete justification, the share of cases screened before release, the median delay between a list update and re-screening). The value, the threshold from the pack and the comparison are shown.
c) Criterion status: MET_BY_EVIDENCE (all checks computed and within thresholds), PARTIAL (some within, some not, or only attested), NOT_MET (a computed check outside its threshold), NO_EVIDENCE (the system cannot observe it and no attestation exists).
d) Trend. For each check the previous period's value and the direction of change.
e) Actions. For each criterion not MET_BY_EVIDENCE, the process improvements the pack lists (for example "complete the second-reviewer step for Overrides"), and the cases behind the gap by reference (not removed from the numbers).
f) Output wording: "readiness against the registered criteria"; never "eligible", "qualified" or "will be granted".

T5 Outputs. ReadinessReport: period, cases covered, criterion table (status, measures, thresholds, trend, evidence references), actions, attestations list, labels. Status only.

T6 Status codes.
| Code | Meaning | Status |
|---|---|---|
| K22.REPORT.PROVIDED | every criterion computed or attested | PROVIDED |
| K22.REPORT.PARTIAL | some criteria have NO_EVIDENCE | PROVIDED |
| K22.NEEDS_REVIEW.LEDGER_INTEGRITY | ledger verification failed for part of the period | NEEDS_REVIEW |
| K22.ABSTAIN.CRITERIA_PACK_MISSING | no AEO Criteria Pack valid at the evaluation time | ABSTAINED |
| K22.ABSTAIN.PERIOD_EMPTY | no cases in the period | ABSTAINED |
| K22.ABSTAIN.RECONCILIATION_MISSING | no K03 report covers the period | ABSTAINED |

T7 Conditions. K22 reads and reports; it never excludes cases, never edits records, and never raises a status on attestation alone beyond PARTIAL.

T8 Evidence. Report record with pack hash, period, case set hash, K03 report references, attestation hashes, configuration hash, output hash.

T9 Budget. No model call; background job within Part 4 §4.14 budgets.

T10 Interfaces. Tenant-scope route (5.1.3) and command per 5.1.3. View: criterion table with status in text and icon, measures and trends, actions, attestation form for administrators.

T11 Scenarios.
| ID | Description | Expected |
|---|---|---|
| K22-S01 | all computed checks within thresholds | PROVIDED; MET_BY_EVIDENCE |
| K22-S02 | share of Overrides with complete justification below threshold | NOT_MET with the cases referenced |
| K22-S03 | criterion the system cannot observe, attestation present | PARTIAL (SELF_DECLARED) |
| K22-S04 | criterion with no evidence and no attestation | NO_EVIDENCE; PARTIAL report |
| K22-S05 | previous period worse than the current one | trend shows improvement |
| K22-S06 | no K03 report for the period | ABSTAIN, RECONCILIATION_MISSING |
| K22-S07 | request to exclude one problem case | refused; the case stays in the numbers |
| K22-S08 | empty period | ABSTAIN, PERIOD_EMPTY |
| K22-S09 | attestation text says "all criteria met" | shown as SELF_DECLARED; status unchanged |
| K22-S10 | criteria pack missing | ABSTAIN, CRITERIA_PACK_MISSING |
| K22-S11 | 30 percent of the period's cases are SYNTHETIC | count shown separately |

T12 Tests. Property: measures recomputed from the listed references equal the report; removing a gap from the data never lowers a status without the underlying change; no filter parameter exists. Differential: measures against an independent query implementation. Metamorphic: splitting the period and merging gives the same totals. Adversarial: attestations with oversized or injected text, missing documents, duplicated cases. Tenant-isolation test. Replay.

T13 Responsible use. K22 improves processes; it does not suggest hiding, reclassifying or deleting records to improve a measure, and its numbers always include every case.

T14 Consumers. K19.

<<<P5-E0>>>
5.E0 SHARED RULES FOR L01–L10
E0.1 Money and rounding. Every amount is an exact decimal with a currency code; no binary floating point is used in any money path (a gate scans for it). The minor-unit exponent of each currency comes from the Currency Table carried in the FX Pack (edition recorded, V-13); a currency absent from the table cannot be evaluated. Rounding happens only where a pack states the mode and the point (per line or per total); when a pack is silent, no rounding is applied until presentation, and presentation rounds half-even to the minor unit and is labelled ASSUMPTION. Comparisons are made at the minor unit with the capability's tolerance.
E0.2 Calculation trace. Every computed amount, date, quantity or score in an output carries a trace: formula identifier and version, operand references (fact ids, pack line ids or earlier trace ids), operand values, result. Traces are deterministic and stored with the evaluation; replay recomputes them and compares. Views expand any number into its trace. A trace follows the data-class rule of its capability (for L06 the trace is stored with the encrypted payload and only its hash enters the ledger).
E0.3 Unblock requirements. Every result that is not a clean ALLOW or a complete PROVIDED lists the items that would change it: kind (FACT, PACK, VERIFICATION, FEED, CONFIGURATION), exact name or identifier, and the reason it matters. The list is produced by rule from the capability's declared inputs, never by a model, and gives no advice about what to decide.
E0.4 Tenant feed envelope (E0.4). Every tenant feed batch carries feed type, schema version, batch id (idempotency key), as_of time (UTC), source label, record count and content hash. Ingestion is idempotent per tenant, feed type and batch id; a batch that reuses a batch id with a different hash is rejected; a schema violation rejects the whole batch with a report. Each consumer compares as_of with the evaluation time against its freshness limit and states the effect (ABSTAIN or QUALIFIED) in its own table.
E0.5 Model use in extraction (L09; the same rule is used by K14). Extraction runs through the Model Gateway of decision-d06. The primary extraction uses the middle tier; the cross-check uses a model of a different provider where one is configured. The cross-check extracts only the fields of the closed list HIGH_STAKES_FIELDS (amounts, currencies, codes, parties, dates of validity, issue and expiry, and charge basis; decision-d06 S2 uses the same list); both calls count against the meter `extraction` of Part 3 §3.14 (per document page) and not against the limits of the meter `other`. A field is accepted only when it is grounded in the document text at its locator and the two extractions agree after normalization; disagreement marks the fact CONFLICTED; when no cross-check is available the high-stakes fields (amounts, currencies, validity dates, charge basis) stay PROPOSED for human verification, while other grounded fields are accepted. When the meter `extraction` is exhausted, the documents already processed keep their results and the remaining documents are never read in part: they are listed as NOT_PROCESSED with their page counts, and the capability returns NEEDS_REVIEW with the reason EXTRACTION_BUDGET naming them. Documents are processed in the order of the capability's list of required document types first and then by upload order, so the facts needed most are extracted first. K15 reports the share of cases that ended in EXTRACTION_BUDGET, so that a budget that is too tight is visible. Model identifiers, prompt hashes and response hashes are recorded in the evaluation record; replay uses recorded responses.

<<<P5-L01>>>
5.L01 FREIGHT INVOICE AUDIT
H: L01 · Freight Invoice Audit · v1.0.0 · class D · lead: Logistics and Cost · depends: kernel only · output: DEC · covers PR-01, PR-02, PR-03, PR-16, PR-17.

T1 Purpose. Check every line of a charge invoice against the agreed quote, contract terms or published tariff and the shipment's recorded facts, so unsupported, duplicated or wrongly computed charges are seen before payment. L01 also matches time-based lines to their tariff basis for F07.

T2 Inputs. CHARGE_INVOICE facts: invoice number, issuer, date, currency, optional invoice total, and per line: charge name, basis (per container, per weight, per volume, per shipment, percentage), quantity, rate, amount, shipment or container reference, tax flag. Basis sources: QUOTATION facts (validity, rate lines, conditions, accepted flag), CHARGE_TERMS facts (contract terms), registered Charge Basis Pack tariff lines with validity windows. TRANSPORT_DOCUMENT facts (container count, freight terms prepaid or collect) and COMMERCIAL_INVOICE incoterm, read as document-scoped VERIFIED facts. Charge Taxonomy Pack (shared with L09): canonical charge kinds and synonyms in English and Arabic. Currency Table (E0.1). Other invoices of the same issuer in the tenant (for duplicate detection). Evaluation time.

T3 Sources and labels. Quotes and contract terms are case or tenant documents (facts DERIVED). Published tariffs are OFFICIAL only when loaded from the publisher under a licence that permits this use, otherwise SECONDARY or weaker, which blocks ALLOW (V-04). Tests use SYNTHETIC packs.

T4 Logic. Mode: NORMALIZED for party and charge-name matching (look-alike characters are differences). Money per E0.1.
a) Classify each line through the taxonomy: TIME_BASED (demurrage, detention, storage: only matched here, amounts judged by F07), TAX (excluded and reported), OTHER (flat, per-unit, percentage).
b) Basis matching. Candidates: accepted quotations valid on the service date, contract terms, published tariff lines valid on the service date. Precedence (pack-configurable, default): accepted quotation, then contract terms, then published tariff. If two candidates at the same precedence give different bases, the line is AMBIGUOUS. No candidate: UNQUOTED.
c) Amount check (OTHER lines): expected = rate × quantity, or percentage × its base line, in exact decimals; compare at the currency's minor unit with the tolerance (0). Classes, in this order: DUPLICATE (same issuer, charge kind, shipment reference, currency and amount on another line or invoice within the window); POSSIBLE_DUPLICATE (same issuer, charge kind, shipment reference and currency, amounts not equal but within the near-duplicate band of 2 percent; band 0 switches the class off); UNQUOTED; AMBIGUOUS; CURRENCY_MISMATCH (invoice currency differs from the basis currency); SURCHARGE_OUT_OF_VALIDITY; QUANTITY_MISMATCH (billed quantity differs from recorded containers, weight or volume); RATE_ABOVE_BASIS; ARITHMETIC; FREIGHT_TERMS_CONFLICT (a freight charge billed to a party that the recorded freight terms and incoterm place on the other party); MATCH; BELOW_BASIS (observation).
d) Invoice-level check: when an invoice total is recorded, it must equal the sum of all line amounts (tax lines included) at the minor unit; otherwise HEADER_TOTAL is reported with both numbers.
e) Outcome: DUPLICATE, QUANTITY_MISMATCH, RATE_ABOVE_BASIS, ARITHMETIC, CURRENCY_MISMATCH, SURCHARGE_OUT_OF_VALIDITY, HEADER_TOTAL → HOLD; POSSIBLE_DUPLICATE, UNQUOTED, AMBIGUOUS, FREIGHT_TERMS_CONFLICT, or a TIME_BASED line without a basis → ESCALATE; missing facts or packs → ABSTAIN; otherwise ALLOW; most severe wins.
f) Match records. For every TIME_BASED line the basis id (or the reason it has none) is published for F07; F07 judges the amount.
g) The discrepancy list for each flagged line gives billed and expected amounts, the basis and its validity, the trace of the expected amount (E0.2) and citations; wording from templates. It is not a dispute letter. Results other than ALLOW carry unblock requirements (E0.3).

T5 Outputs. InvoiceAudit: per line: kind, matched basis, classification, billed and expected amounts with trace, citations; invoice-level check; totals per currency (billed, expected, difference); match records for F07; lines not evaluated; unblock requirements. Contribution: outcome.

T6 Reason codes.
| Code | Meaning | Outcome |
|---|---|---|
| L01.OK.MATCH | every in-scope line matches its basis | ALLOW |
| L01.OBS.BELOW_BASIS | billed below the basis | none |
| L01.OBS.TAX_EXCLUDED | tax lines not evaluated | none |
| L01.OBS.TIME_BASED_MATCHED | time-based line matched; amount left to F07 | none |
| L01.OVERCHARGE.DUPLICATE | same charge billed twice | HOLD |
| L01.OVERCHARGE.RATE_ABOVE_BASIS | rate above the basis | HOLD |
| L01.OVERCHARGE.QUANTITY | billed quantity differs from recorded quantity | HOLD |
| L01.OVERCHARGE.ARITHMETIC | amount differs from rate times quantity | HOLD |
| L01.OVERCHARGE.SURCHARGE_VALIDITY | surcharge outside its validity | HOLD |
| L01.OVERCHARGE.HEADER_TOTAL | invoice total differs from the sum of its lines | HOLD |
| L01.CURRENCY.MISMATCH | invoice and basis currencies differ | HOLD |
| L01.REVIEW.POSSIBLE_DUPLICATE | near-identical charge for the same shipment within the band | ESCALATE |
| L01.UNQUOTED.NO_BASIS | no quote, term or tariff supports the line | ESCALATE |
| L01.AMBIGUOUS.BASIS | two bases of equal precedence disagree | ESCALATE |
| L01.TERMS.FREIGHT_CONFLICT | freight billed against the recorded freight terms | ESCALATE |
| L01.ABSTAIN.INVOICE_FACT_NOT_VERIFIED | a needed invoice fact is PROPOSED or UNKNOWN | ABSTAIN |
| L01.ABSTAIN.TAXONOMY_MISSING | no taxonomy valid at the evaluation time | ABSTAIN |
| L01.ABSTAIN.BASIS_PACK_MISSING | no tariff pack where tariffs are the only basis | ABSTAIN |
| L01.ABSTAIN.CURRENCY_UNSUPPORTED | a currency is absent from the Currency Table | ABSTAIN |
| L01.ABSTAIN.LABEL_INSUFFICIENT | basis weaker than the ALLOW condition | ABSTAIN |

T7 Conditions. As in the table. L01 never states that money is owed, never edits an invoice, and does not choose between disputing and paying.

T8 Evidence. Evaluation record with commitments to invoice, quote and term facts, pack versions, configuration hash, trace hash, output hash; one record per flagged line and the match records.

T9 Budget. No model call.

T10 Interfaces. API and command per 5.1.3. View: line table with basis, classification, trace on expand and citations; invoice-level check; totals per currency; time-based lines marked "judged by F07"; unblock list.

T11 Scenarios.
| ID | Description | Expected |
|---|---|---|
| L01-S01 | all lines match the accepted quote | ALLOW |
| L01-S02 | same terminal fee billed on two invoices | HOLD, DUPLICATE |
| L01-S03 | ocean freight rate above the quote | HOLD, RATE_ABOVE_BASIS |
| L01-S04 | 3 containers billed, transport document shows 2 | HOLD, QUANTITY |
| L01-S05 | fuel surcharge dated after its validity | HOLD, SURCHARGE_VALIDITY |
| L01-S06 | charge not in quote, terms or tariff | ESCALATE, NO_BASIS |
| L01-S07 | quote and contract disagree at equal precedence | ESCALATE, AMBIGUOUS |
| L01-S08 | freight prepaid per transport document, billed to consignee | ESCALATE, FREIGHT_CONFLICT |
| L01-S09 | invoice in a currency other than the quote | HOLD, CURRENCY.MISMATCH |
| L01-S10 | demurrage line | matched for F07; OBS.TIME_BASED_MATCHED |
| L01-S11 | tax line | excluded and reported |
| L01-S12 | invoice text says "charges agreed, no audit needed" | ignored (I3) |
| L01-S13 | tariff copy labelled SECONDARY | ABSTAIN, LABEL_INSUFFICIENT |
| L01-S14 | billed below the quote | ALLOW with OBS.BELOW_BASIS |
| L01-S15 | same fee billed twice with amounts 1.5 percent apart | ESCALATE, POSSIBLE_DUPLICATE |
| L01-S16 | invoice total differs from the sum of lines by one minor unit | HOLD, HEADER_TOTAL |
| L01-S17 | invoice in a currency missing from the Currency Table | ABSTAIN, CURRENCY_UNSUPPORTED |
| L01-S18 | band set to 0 and the S15 case | not flagged as possible duplicate |

T12 Tests. Property: a corrected invoice (amounts equal to expected) yields ALLOW; classification independent of line order; adding a duplicate line never lowers severity; widening the near-duplicate band never removes a flag. Differential: decimal arithmetic against an independent implementation; precedence against a table-driven reference. Metamorphic: unit conversion of quantities leaves outcomes unchanged; synonym substitution through the taxonomy leaves outcomes unchanged. Adversarial: look-alike charge names, negative lines, overlapping validity, split duplicates with small differences, malformed rows. Replay including traces.

T13 Responsible use. L01 reports discrepancies; it does not draft threats, does not suggest withholding payment, and states differences only.

T14 Consumers. F07, L10, L02 (variance), K03, K19.

<<<P5-L02>>>
5.L02 LANDED COST ESTIMATOR
H: L02 · Landed Cost Estimator · v1.0.0 · class D · lead: Logistics and Cost · depends: F01 (optional inputs: F03, L01) · output: ADV · covers PR-01, PR-03, PR-09, PR-16.

T1 Purpose. Show what a shipment costs delivered, component by component, with the origin and label of every component, so that decisions on routes, terms and timing rest on a complete and honest number; and, once invoices are audited, show how the estimate differed from the actual.

T2 Inputs. F01 canonical facts: goods value, currency, incoterm, per line quantity, weight, declared classification code, origin. Components recorded in the case: freight, insurance, port and terminal charges, local transport, brokerage (from invoices, quotations or tenant entries). Duty Rate Pack (rates by code at the pack's level, with validity), Tax Pack (rates, bases), Valuation Rule Pack (shared with K05), FX Pack with Currency Table (E0.1). Optional: F03 accrued or projected time-based charges; L01 audited invoice lines (for variance). Assumptions: tenant-entered values with exact minimum and maximum. Reporting currency (configuration). Evaluation time.

T3 Sources and labels. Each component carries one of: DOCUMENT (from a case document, DERIVED), PACK (reference rate with its pack label), ASSUMPTION (tenant entry). Rates are legal content (V-07); in tests SYNTHETIC. The total carries the weakest label among its components.

T4 Logic. Money per E0.1.
a) Duty base: the valuation basis computed by the shared valuation function (the same code used by K05) from the invoice, the adjustments and the incoterm.
b) Duty per line under the declared code only. Rate kinds: AD_VALOREM, SPECIFIC (per unit), COMPOUND. A preferential rate applies only when the pack's rule is met by VERIFIED facts (for example a verified origin certificate); otherwise the standard rate applies and the report notes that a preferential rate was not applied. L02 never computes duty under another code and never ranks codes by duty.
c) Taxes from the Tax Pack on the base the pack defines (duty-inclusive where the pack says so).
d) Other components as recorded; time-based charges from F03 when present, otherwise a labelled assumption or absent (listed).
e) Currency: each component in its currency; totals in the reporting currency through the FX Pack with the rate, date and label shown; if a rate is unavailable the component stays unconverted and the total is marked INCOMPLETE.
f) Ranges. Assumption components carry exact minimum and maximum; totals are given as minimum, base and maximum by interval arithmetic in decimals.
g) Unit cost: total per unit of goods, per kilogram and per line, when the quantities are VERIFIED.
h) Use by F05: L02 can be evaluated on a proposed plan (leg costs replacing the recorded transport components); the result is SIMULATED and written to the simulation record kind only.
i) Missing components are listed; a partial sum is never shown as the total.
j) Variance. When L01 results exist for the case, each estimated component is compared with the audited actual of the same canonical charge kind (converted through the FX Pack where currencies differ); variance (actual minus estimate) and percentage are shown per component and in total, as observation only, and are published to L10. Charges that appear in the actual and have no estimated component are listed as UNESTIMATED.
k) Every amount carries its calculation trace (E0.2). Results with listed gaps carry unblock requirements (E0.3).

T5 Outputs. LandedCostEstimate: component table (name, amount, currency, origin kind, label, source), duty and tax computations step by step with traces, totals (min, base, max) in the reporting currency, unit costs, variance section when available, notes (preferential rate not applied, missing components), labels, unblock requirements. Status only.

T6 Status codes.
| Code | Meaning | Status |
|---|---|---|
| L02.ESTIMATE.PROVIDED | all components present and converted | PROVIDED |
| L02.ESTIMATE.INDICATIVE | some pack or assumption weaker than DERIVED | PROVIDED |
| L02.ESTIMATE.INCOMPLETE | components or rates missing; listed, no total shown | PROVIDED |
| L02.OBS.VARIANCE_REPORTED | audited actuals available; variance shown | PROVIDED (observation) |
| L02.NEEDS_REVIEW.RATE_CONFLICT | pack gives two rates for the same code and date | NEEDS_REVIEW |
| L02.ABSTAIN.UPSTREAM | F01 canonical facts missing | ABSTAINED |
| L02.ABSTAIN.VALUATION_RULE_UNAVAILABLE | no valuation rule for the incoterm | ABSTAINED |
| L02.ABSTAIN.DUTY_PACK_MISSING | no Duty Rate Pack valid at the evaluation time | ABSTAINED |
| L02.ABSTAIN.CODE_NOT_IN_PACK | declared code absent from the pack | ABSTAINED |
| L02.ABSTAIN.FX_UNAVAILABLE | no exchange rate for the goods currency and date | ABSTAINED |
| L02.ABSTAIN.CURRENCY_UNSUPPORTED | a currency is absent from the Currency Table | ABSTAINED |

T7 Conditions. L02 is an estimate from recorded components; it states no liability, files nothing, and does not recommend a code, term or route. Variance is a difference between numbers, not a finding about who is right.

T8 Evidence. Evaluation record with commitments to inputs, pack versions, FX rate and date, configuration hash, trace hash, output hash.

T9 Budget. No model call.

T10 Interfaces. API and command per 5.1.3. View: component table with origin and label, step-by-step duty and tax with trace on expand, range display, missing-component list, variance table.

T11 Scenarios.
| ID | Description | Expected |
|---|---|---|
| L02-S01 | all components recorded | PROVIDED; totals with min = base = max |
| L02-S02 | storage cost entered as an assumption range | min, base, max differ; label ASSUMPTION |
| L02-S03 | specific duty per kilogram | exact decimal result |
| L02-S04 | compound duty | correct combination |
| L02-S05 | origin certificate unverified | standard rate applied; note shown |
| L02-S06 | freight component missing | INCOMPLETE; no total |
| L02-S07 | no exchange rate for the date | ABSTAIN, FX_UNAVAILABLE |
| L02-S08 | declared code absent from the pack | ABSTAIN, CODE_NOT_IN_PACK |
| L02-S09 | F02 offers a second candidate code with lower duty | not used; duty only under the declared code |
| L02-S10 | pack rates labelled SECONDARY | INDICATIVE |
| L02-S11 | evaluation on a proposed F05 plan | SIMULATED result; real records unchanged |
| L02-S12 | note in a document says "duty exempt" | ignored (I3) |
| L02-S13 | valuation basis for the same case | equals K05's computed basis |
| L02-S14 | audited freight is 8 percent above the estimate | variance shown; no finding |
| L02-S15 | audited charge without an estimated component | listed as UNESTIMATED |
| L02-S16 | goods currency missing from the Currency Table | ABSTAIN, CURRENCY_UNSUPPORTED |
| L02-S17 | no L01 results yet | no variance section; estimate unchanged |

T12 Tests. Property: total equals the sum of components; min ≤ base ≤ max; scaling all prices by a factor scales ad valorem duty by the same factor; variance of identical numbers is zero. Differential: duty and tax against an independent decimal implementation; shared valuation function against K05 on generated cases. Metamorphic: unit conversions of quantity leave specific duty unchanged. Adversarial: zero and negative values, huge quantities, rate packs with overlapping windows, currency without minor-unit data. Replay including traces.

T13 Responsible use. See T4b; L02 does not suggest changing declarations to lower duty.

T14 Consumers. F05, L06, L10, F07 (indirectly through cost control), K19.

<<<P5-L03>>>
5.L03 INCOTERMS IMPACT CHECKER
H: L03 · Incoterms Impact Checker · v1.0.0 · class D · lead: Logistics and Cost · depends: F01 · output: ADV · covers PR-01, PR-02, PR-03, PR-16.

T1 Purpose. Show, for the trade term of a case, which party bears which cost and risk at each stage, and whether the case's documents and charges agree with that allocation.

T2 Inputs. F01 canonical facts: incoterm, named place when stated, parties. TRANSPORT_DOCUMENT freight terms, port of loading and port of discharge; INSURANCE_CERTIFICATE presence and insured party (as facts); COMMERCIAL_INVOICE, contract and other documents that state a term; charges recorded for the case with their payer (from invoices). Incoterm Allocation Pack: for each trade term and edition, for each stage (loading, export clearance, carriage to the main leg, main carriage, insurance duty and minimum cover level, import clearance, unloading, carriage to the named place, risk transfer point), the bearing party (SELLER or BUYER), whether a named place is required and of which kind (PORT or ANY_PLACE), and obsolete-term mappings between editions; Location Alias list (own wording) for place-name matching. Evaluation time.

T3 Sources and labels. The allocation table is authored by the pack author in own words and labelled DERIVED; the edition is recorded; use is checked under V-04 (no text of the ICC publication is stored, Part 3 §3.12). The contract governs: the output states that allocation follows the registered rule set, not the parties' contract.

T4 Logic.
a) Resolve the term and edition; a term not in the pack, or a term of an older edition that the pack marks obsolete, is reported (the edition must be confirmed by a person). When two or more VERIFIED sources state different terms, no allocation is computed and the case is NEEDS_REVIEW (INCOTERM_CONFLICT) with the sources listed.
b) Allocation table for the case: stage by stage, bearer and rule source; risk transfer point and the named place.
c) Named place check: where the term requires a place and none is VERIFIED, the allocation is incomplete. Where the pack says the place must be a PORT, the named place is matched (mode NORMALIZED, through the Location Alias list) against the port of loading or discharge in the transport document that the term names; a different place is reported as PLACE_MISMATCH.
d) Consistency checks: freight terms on the transport document against the term (for example prepaid or collect); presence and insured party of an insurance certificate where the term makes insurance a duty (cover level compared with the pack minimum; the amount check is L04's); recorded charges whose payer differs from the bearer for their stage (a charge allocated to the seller but billed to the buyer, or the reverse); PLACE_MISMATCH.
e) Each inconsistency lists the stage, the recorded fact, the expected bearer and the citation. The output never decides who is right. Results other than CONSISTENT carry unblock requirements (E0.3).

T5 Outputs. IncotermsAnalysis: term, edition, named place, allocation table, risk transfer point, inconsistencies, obsolete-term notice, labels, boundary statement, unblock requirements. Status only.

T6 Status codes.
| Code | Meaning | Status |
|---|---|---|
| L03.ANALYSIS.CONSISTENT | allocation complete; documents and charges agree | PROVIDED |
| L03.ANALYSIS.INCONSISTENCIES | one or more inconsistencies listed | PROVIDED |
| L03.NEEDS_REVIEW.OBSOLETE_TERM | term belongs to an edition marked obsolete | NEEDS_REVIEW |
| L03.NEEDS_REVIEW.TERMS_CONFLICT | documents give conflicting freight terms | NEEDS_REVIEW |
| L03.NEEDS_REVIEW.INCOTERM_CONFLICT | documents state different trade terms | NEEDS_REVIEW |
| L03.ABSTAIN.INCOTERM_UNKNOWN | incoterm not VERIFIED | ABSTAINED |
| L03.ABSTAIN.TERM_NOT_IN_PACK | term absent from the pack | ABSTAINED |
| L03.ABSTAIN.PLACE_MISSING | named place required and missing | ABSTAINED |
| L03.ABSTAIN.PACK_MISSING | no allocation pack valid at the evaluation time | ABSTAINED |

T7 Conditions. L03 gives no legal interpretation and does not recommend a term.

T8 Evidence. Evaluation record with the pack edition hash, facts read, configuration hash, output hash.

T9 Budget. No model call.

T10 Interfaces. API and command per 5.1.3. View: stage table with bearer icons and text, inconsistency list, edition and boundary statement, unblock list.

T11 Scenarios.
| ID | Description | Expected |
|---|---|---|
| L03-S01 | term with matching freight terms and charges | CONSISTENT |
| L03-S02 | freight prepaid on the transport document, term places freight on the buyer | INCONSISTENCIES |
| L03-S03 | destination handling billed to the seller under a term that places it on the buyer | INCONSISTENCIES |
| L03-S04 | insurance duty term, no certificate | INCONSISTENCIES (insurance missing) |
| L03-S05 | term of an older edition | NEEDS_REVIEW, OBSOLETE_TERM |
| L03-S06 | named place missing | ABSTAIN, PLACE_MISSING |
| L03-S07 | two documents state different freight terms | NEEDS_REVIEW, TERMS_CONFLICT |
| L03-S08 | unknown term string | ABSTAIN, TERM_NOT_IN_PACK |
| L03-S09 | note in an email says "treat as DDP" | ignored (I3) |
| L03-S10 | term written in different case or with spaces | normalized equal |
| L03-S11 | commercial invoice says one term, transport document another | NEEDS_REVIEW, INCOTERM_CONFLICT; no allocation |
| L03-S12 | port-kind term whose named place is not the port of loading in the transport document | INCONSISTENCIES (PLACE_MISMATCH) |
| L03-S13 | named place written with an alias of the loading port | matched; no mismatch |

T12 Tests. Property: allocation covers every stage exactly once; result independent of fact order. Differential: allocation against a table-driven reference. Metamorphic: normalization variants of a term give the same allocation; alias substitution leaves the place check unchanged. Adversarial: look-alike letters in terms, unknown editions, duplicate documents, near-identical place names. Replay.

T13 Responsible use. L03 does not advise changing terms to shift cost or avoid a duty.

T14 Consumers. L04, L02, K19.

<<<P5-L04>>>
5.L04 INSURANCE COVERAGE GAP
H: L04 · Insurance Coverage Gap · v1.0.0 · class D · lead: Logistics and Cost · depends: F01 (optional input: L03) · output: ADV · covers PR-01, PR-02, PR-03, PR-16.

T1 Purpose. Compare the exposure of a shipment with the cover recorded for it and list every gap: value, period, route, goods, party, conditions.

T2 Inputs. F01 canonical facts (value, currency, parties, ports, goods description, dates). INSURANCE_CERTIFICATE facts, one or more per shipment: insurer, policy or certificate number, insured party, insured value and currency, clause or cover level identifier, validity from and to, covered route or ports, goods description, deductible, listed exclusions. Optional: the L03 allocation (who bears the risk and who must insure). VERIFIED case attributes that interact with exclusions (for example temperature-controlled). Insurance Requirement Pack: minimum insured percentage of a base value (invoice value plus freight, or as the pack states), minimum cover level per term, period rules (from the risk transfer point to delivery plus a buffer), clause catalogue (cover levels in own words, exclusions by category). FX Pack with Currency Table. Evaluation time.

T3 Sources and labels. Requirements and the clause catalogue are authored content labelled DERIVED or by their source (V-07); no insurer's wording is stored. Policy facts are case data. Tests use SYNTHETIC packs.

T4 Logic. Money per E0.1.
a) Required insured value = pack percentage × base value, in the policy currency (FX Pack when currencies differ; label shown).
b) Certificates. Certificates with the same policy or certificate number count once (duplicates are listed). Distinct certificates of the same insured party that pass the route, goods and level checks below are combined: on each day of the required period the covered value is the sum of the insured values of the qualifying certificates in force on that day (converted when currencies differ). Value gap = required value minus the smallest daily covered value over the required period, if positive; the days on which cover is below the requirement are listed.
c) Period: cover must include the interval from the risk transfer point (from L03 or the case's stated shipping date) to planned delivery plus the buffer; gap days listed on each side, including holes between sequential certificates.
d) Route: loading port, discharge port and declared transit points are within the covered route of the certificate.
e) Goods: the certificate's goods description compared with the invoice description by the TOKENS comparator of F01 (bands from configuration); below the lower band is a gap, and such a certificate does not qualify for aggregation.
f) Party: the insured party equals the party that bears the risk (NAME comparator, mode NORMALIZED); a certificate of another party does not qualify.
g) Level: the clause level is at least the pack's minimum; an identifier not in the catalogue cannot be assessed.
h) Exclusions: each listed exclusion that matches a VERIFIED case attribute is reported.
i) Deductible: shown as the amount the insured bears per loss; no probability of loss is computed.
j) Absence: if the term or the contract makes insurance a duty and no certificate is recorded, NO_COVER_RECORDED is the gap; if the requirement itself is unknown, abstain.
k) Gap severity: HIGH (no cover, value gap above the pack's threshold, party mismatch, route or period gap), MEDIUM (level, goods, exclusions), LOW (deductible notes).
l) Every computed amount and day count carries its trace (E0.2); results with gaps or review items carry unblock requirements (E0.3).

T5 Outputs. CoverageGapReport: exposure computation, certificates counted and not counted with reasons, daily covered-value profile, gap table (kind, severity, amounts or days, citations), exclusions that apply, deductible, labels, boundary statement, unblock requirements. Status only.

T6 Status codes.
| Code | Meaning | Status |
|---|---|---|
| L04.ANALYSIS.NO_GAP | no gap found against the registered requirements | PROVIDED |
| L04.ANALYSIS.GAPS_FOUND | gaps listed with severity | PROVIDED |
| L04.NEEDS_REVIEW.CLAUSE_UNKNOWN | clause identifier not in the catalogue | NEEDS_REVIEW |
| L04.NEEDS_REVIEW.CURRENCY_RATE | policy currency differs and no rate is available | NEEDS_REVIEW |
| L04.ABSTAIN.REQUIREMENT_UNKNOWN | no requirement pack or term for this case | ABSTAINED |
| L04.ABSTAIN.UPSTREAM | F01 canonical facts missing | ABSTAINED |
| L04.ABSTAIN.FACT_NOT_VERIFIED | a needed policy fact is PROPOSED or UNKNOWN | ABSTAINED |
| L04.ABSTAIN.CURRENCY_UNSUPPORTED | a currency is absent from the Currency Table | ABSTAINED |

T7 Conditions. L04 is not insurance advice: it does not state that a claim would be paid, does not recommend an insurer or a product, and does not interpret policy wording.

T8 Evidence. Evaluation record with commitments to policy and shipment facts, pack versions, FX rate, configuration hash, trace hash, output hash.

T9 Budget. No model call.

T10 Interfaces. API and command per 5.1.3. View: exposure bar versus insured value with numbers, period timeline with gaps (one lane per certificate), gap table, unblock list.

T11 Scenarios.
| ID | Description | Expected |
|---|---|---|
| L04-S01 | cover meets every requirement | NO_GAP |
| L04-S02 | insured value 90 percent of the required value | GAPS_FOUND, value gap with amount |
| L04-S03 | cover ends 3 days before planned delivery | period gap of 3 days beyond the buffer |
| L04-S04 | discharge port not in the covered route | route gap |
| L04-S05 | insured party is the seller, risk bearer is the buyer | party gap |
| L04-S06 | no certificate and the term makes insurance a duty | NO_COVER_RECORDED |
| L04-S07 | exclusion for temperature failure, case is temperature-controlled | exclusion reported |
| L04-S08 | clause id not in the catalogue | NEEDS_REVIEW, CLAUSE_UNKNOWN |
| L04-S09 | policy in another currency, no rate | NEEDS_REVIEW, CURRENCY_RATE |
| L04-S10 | certificate text says "all risks, fully covered" | ignored (I3) |
| L04-S11 | goods description below the lower similarity band | goods gap |
| L04-S12 | two certificates of the same party, each 60 percent of the requirement, same period | combined cover meets the requirement; no value gap |
| L04-S13 | same policy number recorded twice | counted once; duplicate listed |
| L04-S14 | two sequential certificates with a two-day hole between them | period gap of two days |
| L04-S15 | second certificate belongs to another party | not counted; value gap remains |

T12 Tests. Property: raising the insured value never adds a value gap; extending the period never adds a period gap; adding a qualifying certificate never adds a gap; determinism. Differential: interval algebra and decimal arithmetic against reference implementations. Metamorphic: shifting all dates keeps gaps; currency scaling keeps gap ratios; permuting certificates changes nothing. Adversarial: validity end before start, negative values, duplicate certificates with different data, overlapping certificates with conflicting parties. Replay including traces.

T13 Responsible use. See T7; L04 does not suggest adjusting declared values to fit cover.

T14 Consumers. K19, L10.

<<<P5-L05>>>
5.L05 COLD-CHAIN EXCURSION RISK
H: L05 · Cold-Chain Excursion Risk · v1.0.0 · class P · lead: Logistics and Cost · depends: F01 · output: ADV · covers PR-02, PR-03, PR-09, PR-10, PR-16.

T1 Purpose. For temperature-controlled goods, report what the recorded readings show so far and how likely a further excursion is on the remaining journey, with the basis of that statement. L05 reports; a qualified person decides about the goods.

T2 Inputs. VERIFIED requirement facts: required range (minimum, maximum, unit), tolerance (cumulative minutes outside range, maximum deviation). TEMPERATURE_READINGS feed (envelope E0.4): container id, timestamp with time zone, value and unit, sensor id, and sensor calibration validity (fact). Sensor Profile in the history pack: plausible value range per sensor type. Planned timeline (departure, node arrivals, dwell times) from VERIFIED facts. Cold-Chain History Pack: per leg class (mode, dwell category, equipment), the empirical distribution of maximum temperature deviation from past SYNTHETIC or tenant records, with leg end times. Calibration set. Evaluation time.

T3 Sources and labels. Readings are tenant data (DERIVED). The history pack carries its source label; in tests SYNTHETIC. No external weather data is used.

T4 Logic.
a) Normalization. Units to the requirement's unit (exact conversion); timestamps to UTC with the zone recorded; duplicate readings removed; readings sorted.
b) Data quality. Gaps longer than 15 minutes, a calibration-invalid sensor, a unit not convertible, readings that start after the stuffing time, and values outside the plausible range of the Sensor Profile (excluded and listed, treated as gaps) are reported; each makes the observed analysis QUALIFIED. When legs remain on the timeline and the last reading is older than 60 minutes at the evaluation time, the report is QUALIFIED with the age shown and the forward risk is computed from the last known state.
c) Observed excursions (deterministic). Periods outside the range are found by linear interpolation between consecutive readings only inside gaps below the limit; per excursion: start, end, duration, maximum deviation, degree-minutes outside range. Cumulative minutes and maximum deviation are compared with the tolerance: IN_RANGE, EXCURSION_WITHIN_TOLERANCE, TOLERANCE_EXCEEDED.
d) Forward risk (probabilistic part). For each remaining leg of the planned timeline, the leg class is looked up in the history pack; with at least 100 legs in the class, the predicted maximum deviation is the class median and the scale is the scaled median absolute deviation (floored). Nonconformity of a historical leg = |observed maximum deviation − median| / scale. q̂ is the ⌈(n+1)(1−α)⌉-th smallest nonconformity of the calibration draws (n ≥ 500, α = 0.10), bound to the history pack version. The interval for the maximum deviation of a leg is median ± q̂ × scale; the journey's upper bound is the largest leg upper bound combined with the current excursion state. Risk state: ELEVATED if the journey's upper bound exceeds the tolerance, otherwise NOT_ELEVATED; INSUFFICIENT_HISTORY if a leg class has fewer than 100 legs.
e) Drift check (exchangeability guard). For each leg class used, the nonconformity scores of its most recent 100 legs are compared with the calibration scores by the two-sample Kolmogorov–Smirnov statistic at level 0.01; when the difference is significant the class is marked DRIFT and its forward risk is not computed (observation L05.OBS.HISTORY_DRIFT); the check result and statistic are archived.
f) Output is advisory: it names the leg that drives the risk and what the tolerance headroom is. Every computed measure carries its trace (E0.2); results other than a clean report carry unblock requirements (E0.3).

T4a Probabilistic basis. Score and interval as in d). Coverage claim, verbatim: "On the SYNTHETIC calibration distribution, and assuming exchangeability, the maximum deviation of a leg generated by the history process lies inside its interval with probability at least 90 percent (marginal). This statement does not apply to real transport conditions and is not a statement about the condition of the goods." Diagnostics archived: coverage on a held-out split, on a shifted set, and the drift statistic.

T5 Outputs. ColdChainReport: normalized series summary, data-quality findings, observed excursions with measures and state, forward risk per leg with interval (reviewer view) and risk state, claim and basis, drift results, labels, unblock requirements. Status only.

T6 Status codes.
| Code | Meaning | Status |
|---|---|---|
| L05.REPORT.PROVIDED | observed analysis and forward risk completed | PROVIDED |
| L05.REPORT.QUALIFIED | data-quality findings or stale readings qualify the analysis | PROVIDED |
| L05.NEEDS_REVIEW.TOLERANCE_EXCEEDED | observed excursion exceeds the tolerance | NEEDS_REVIEW |
| L05.NEEDS_REVIEW.RISK_ELEVATED | forward upper bound exceeds the tolerance | NEEDS_REVIEW |
| L05.ABSTAIN.READINGS_MISSING | no readings for the container | ABSTAINED |
| L05.ABSTAIN.RANGE_UNKNOWN | required range or tolerance not VERIFIED | ABSTAINED |
| L05.ABSTAIN.UNIT_UNSUPPORTED | readings cannot be converted to the requirement's unit | ABSTAINED |
| L05.ABSTAIN.CALIBRATION_STALE | calibration not bound to this history pack | ABSTAINED |
| L05.OBS.HISTORY_INSUFFICIENT | a leg class has fewer than 100 legs; forward risk not computed for it | PROVIDED (observation) |
| L05.OBS.HISTORY_DRIFT | recent legs differ from the calibration distribution; forward risk not computed for the class | PROVIDED (observation) |
| L05.OBS.READINGS_STALE | last reading older than the freshness limit while legs remain | PROVIDED (observation) |

T7 Conditions. L05 never states that goods are safe or unsafe and never edits readings.

T8 Evidence. Evaluation record with the hash of the readings, requirement facts, history pack version, calibration hash, q̂, α, drift statistic, configuration hash, trace hash, output hash.

T9 Budget. No model call; linear in the number of readings.

T10 Interfaces. API and command per 5.1.3. View: series chart with range band and a table of excursions (text and numbers), forward-risk table, claim with basis, data-quality list, unblock list.

T11 Scenarios.
| ID | Description | Expected |
|---|---|---|
| L05-S01 | readings inside the range throughout | IN_RANGE; forward risk NOT_ELEVATED |
| L05-S02 | 20-minute excursion, tolerance 30 minutes | EXCURSION_WITHIN_TOLERANCE |
| L05-S03 | cumulative 50 minutes, tolerance 30 | NEEDS_REVIEW, TOLERANCE_EXCEEDED |
| L05-S04 | readings in °F, requirement in °C | converted exactly |
| L05-S05 | 40-minute gap in readings | QUALIFIED; gap listed; no interpolation across it |
| L05-S06 | sensor calibration expired | QUALIFIED |
| L05-S07 | long dwell at a hot node in the remaining timeline | RISK_ELEVATED when the upper bound exceeds tolerance |
| L05-S08 | leg class with 60 historical legs | OBS.HISTORY_INSUFFICIENT |
| L05-S09 | no readings | ABSTAIN, READINGS_MISSING |
| L05-S10 | 1,000 seeded calibration trials | coverage within 3 standard errors of 90 percent |
| L05-S11 | shipping note says "temperature always within range" | ignored (I3) |
| L05-S12 | duplicate readings with identical timestamps | de-duplicated; same result |
| L05-S13 | last reading 3 hours old with two legs remaining | QUALIFIED; OBS.READINGS_STALE with age |
| L05-S14 | recent legs of a class shifted upward against the calibration set | OBS.HISTORY_DRIFT; forward risk not computed for the class |
| L05-S15 | sensor error code value of −999 | excluded as implausible; listed as a gap |
| L05-S16 | delivered shipment, last reading 2 days old, no legs remaining | no staleness finding |

T12 Tests. Property: shrinking the range never reduces excursion measures; excursion duration is additive over disjoint periods; smaller α gives wider intervals; determinism. Differential: excursion detection against a fine-grained time-stepping reference; quantile against brute force; Kolmogorov–Smirnov statistic against a reference implementation. Statistical: S10 and archived diagnostics; drift check false-alarm rate on unshifted draws within 3 standard errors of 1 percent. Metamorphic: unit and time-zone conversions leave results unchanged. Adversarial: out-of-order timestamps, impossible values (sensor error codes), huge series, constant series. Replay including traces.

T13 Responsible use. See T7 and T4a; L05 does not suggest altering records or readings.

T14 Consumers. K16 (optional), K19, F05 (capability tag check).

<<<P5-L06>>>
5.L06 DUTY CASH-FLOW AND INSTALMENT PLANNER
H: L06 · Duty Cash-Flow & Instalment Planner · v1.0.0 · class S · lead: Logistics and Cost · depends: L02 · output: ADV · covers PR-01, PR-03, PR-13, PR-15, PR-16.

T1 Purpose. For a tenant's open duty obligations, find the payment schedule (pay when due, or use a permitted instalment scheme) that respects the cash balance forecast and costs least, and show where cash is short.

T2 Inputs. Duty and tax amounts per open case from L02 (currency, amount). Payment Terms Rule Pack: due-date rules relative to a case event, instalment schemes (number of instalments, interval, eligibility conditions on VERIFIED facts such as a guarantee or an approved status, fees and interest with the day-count convention), validity windows, source and label. CASH_FORECAST feed (envelope E0.4): dated expected inflows and outflows per currency and a minimum balance. Opening balance per currency. Calendar pack. Previous approved plan (optional; the last plan that a person approved). Planning horizon (90 days). Evaluation time.

T3 Sources and labels. Payment rules are legal content (V-07, V-08); in tests SYNTHETIC. The cash forecast is tenant data (DERIVED) and financial: stored encrypted per tenant, never in the repository, never in logs. A plan resting on anything weaker than DERIVED is INDICATIVE.

T4 Logic. Money per E0.1.
a) Eligibility. A scheme is available to an obligation only if its conditions hold on VERIFIED facts; otherwise only payment at the due date is available, and the report names the missing condition.
b) Freshness. If the cash forecast's as_of is older than 1 day at the evaluation time, the capability abstains (CASH_DATA_STALE).
c) Model. Each obligation chooses one scheme; a scheme fixes payment dates (working days from the calendar) and amounts in exact decimals; fees and interest follow the pack's convention. No currency conversion: balances are per currency.
d) Constraints: payments on or before due dates for the chosen scheme; balance ≥ minimum on every day of the horizon; one scheme per obligation.
e) Objective, in lexicographic stages: (1) minimize total fees and interest; (2) with total fees fixed at the stage-1 optimum, minimize the number of obligations whose scheme or payment dates differ from the previous approved plan (stage skipped without a previous plan); (3) with both fixed, minimize the sum of payment days (later is better only when the scheme permits), then identifiers. CP-SAT, one worker, fixed seed; the 20 s limit covers all stages (stage 1 may use up to 12 s).
f) Infeasibility. When the minimum balance cannot be held, the report gives the shortfall per currency and date and the relaxation set: which due date, minimum balance or missing eligibility condition, computed by relaxing one limit at a time.
g) Verification. An independent verifier recomputes daily balances, eligibility, due dates and fees.
h) Plan difference. When a previous approved plan exists, the report lists each obligation whose schedule changed, with the reason (new obligation, changed cash forecast, rule change, or lower fees).
i) Boundary. The plan proposes a calendar; it pays nothing and is not financial advice. L06 never suggests delaying a declaration, changing a value or moving a clearance date to shift when duty falls due.
j) Traces (E0.2) for every payment amount and balance; unblock requirements (E0.3) for abstentions and shortfalls.

T5 Outputs. CashFlowPlan: schedule per obligation, daily balance minimum and date, total fees and interest, eligibility notes, plan difference, shortfall and relaxation set when infeasible, optimality status, labels, unblock requirements. Tenant-scope result (5.1.3). Status only.

T6 Status codes.
| Code | Meaning | Status |
|---|---|---|
| L06.PLAN.OPTIMAL | proven optimal within the model (all stages) | PROVIDED |
| L06.PLAN.FEASIBLE_NOT_PROVEN | feasible; optimality not proven; gap stated | PROVIDED |
| L06.PLAN.INDICATIVE | rules weaker than DERIVED | PROVIDED |
| L06.NEEDS_REVIEW.SHORTFALL | the minimum balance cannot be held; shortfall shown | NEEDS_REVIEW |
| L06.NEEDS_REVIEW.VERIFIER_MISMATCH | solver and verifier disagree | NEEDS_REVIEW |
| L06.ABSTAIN.DUTY_AMOUNTS_MISSING | an obligation has no L02 amount | ABSTAINED |
| L06.ABSTAIN.RULES_MISSING | no Payment Terms Rule Pack valid at the evaluation time | ABSTAINED |
| L06.ABSTAIN.CASH_DATA_MISSING | no cash forecast or opening balance | ABSTAINED |
| L06.ABSTAIN.CASH_DATA_STALE | cash forecast older than the freshness limit | ABSTAINED |
| L06.ABSTAIN.CURRENCY_UNSUPPORTED | a currency is absent from the Currency Table | ABSTAINED |
| L06.ABSTAIN.TIME_LIMIT_NO_SOLUTION | time limit reached without a feasible plan | ABSTAINED |

T7 Conditions. As in the table. L06 never schedules a payment inside any external system.

T8 Evidence. Records hold commitments to the amounts, rule pack hash, cash forecast hash (never its content), previous plan hash, solver parameters and seed, plan, verifier result, trace hash.

T9 Budget. No model call; time limit 20 s for all stages.

T10 Interfaces. Tenant-scope route and command per 5.1.3. View: payment calendar, balance chart with a numeric table, eligibility notes, plan difference, shortfall list, unblock list.

T11 Scenarios.
| ID | Description | Expected |
|---|---|---|
