No capability logic is written. Everything else is forbidden.
Scope paths: kernel/lib/**, schemas/**, tools/capgen/**, tools/trace/**, tools/threat-matrix/**, tools/gate/gates/**, tools/gate/catalogue.toml, contracts.lock, data/synthetic/calibration/**, test-house/lib/**, docs/ledger/P13.md, docs/phase-reports/**, docs/state/**, docs/decisions/**, risk-management/**, CHANGELOG.md, README.md

5. Context Pack: `<<<P3-S3>>>` `<<<P3-S7>>>` `<<<P3-S8>>>` `<<<P3-S13>>>` `<<<P3-S21>>>` `<<<P3-S31>>>` `<<<P4-S4>>>` `<<<P4-S5>>>` `<<<D06-S3>>>` `<<<P6-S3>>>` `<<<P6-S6>>>` `<<<P6-S10>>>` `<<<P6-S12>>>` `<<<P5-E0>>>`.

6. Tasks.

P13-T01 Capability generator (standard layout)
- Files: `tools/capgen/`.
- Steps: generate the identical capability layout (manifest, schemas, rules, fixtures, golden tests, evaluation cases, view stub, documentation stub, registration entry) from a definition file; the architecture test fails a capability that deviates, and the `arch` gate fails when the graph of `depends` over the capability registry (the capability headers of Part 5) or the graph of `depends_on` over the phases (`tools/gate/phases.toml`) contains a cycle, naming the cycle; the registration entry plugs into the uniform route of P10 and the skeleton of P09.
- Accept: tests T-LIB-0001 to T-LIB-0010: regeneration is byte-identical; a deviating layout fails; a generated stub capability runs through the uniform route and the skeleton. Tests T-LIB-0109 to T-LIB-0112: a seeded cycle of two capabilities fails and names both; a seeded cycle through three phases fails; the real registry and the real phase table have no cycle; an F06 input that names a tenant-scope capability fails the registry check.

P13-T02 Money and rounding
- Files: `kernel/lib/money/`.
- Steps: fixed-point decimal values with an ISO 4217 currency, explicit rounding mode and explicit decimal context; conversion only with a recorded rate datum and its label; floating point is rejected by type and by lint; conformance to Part 5 batch E §E0 rules.
- Accept: tests T-LIB-0011 to T-LIB-0030: known-answer vectors for every rounding mode; property tests (addition associativity within context, allocation sums to the total); mixed-currency operations without a rate fail; differential test against an independent decimal implementation.

P13-T03 Calculation trace
- Files: `kernel/lib/calctrace/`.
- Steps: every computed money or quantity value carries a trace tree of inputs, formulas, rule or source ids and labels; the trace is canonical, hashed and referenced from the Decision; label propagation takes the weakest input label.
- Accept: tests T-LIB-0031 to T-LIB-0042: the trace recomputes the value; the label equals the weakest input label; trace hashes are stable.

P13-T04 Unblock-requirement generator
- Files: `kernel/lib/unblock/`.
- Steps: for a HOLD, ABSTAIN or ESCALATE, derive the smallest list of missing or conflicting facts and sources that would let the case proceed, deterministically from reason codes; the list is data and appears in the Passport open items.
- Accept: tests T-LIB-0043 to T-LIB-0052: each reason code maps to requirements; resolving the listed items by fixture changes the outcome as expected; output is order stable.

P13-T05 Tenant feed validator
- Files: `kernel/lib/feeds/`, `schemas/lib/feed_envelope.json`.
- Steps: a feed envelope (tenant, source, label, effective period, schema version, content hash) and validation of tenant-supplied tables; feed data is labelled as tenant-supplied and never raised above that label; the envelope is a frozen contract.
- Accept: tests T-LIB-0053 to T-LIB-0062: invalid, stale and mislabelled feeds are rejected; a feed cannot carry OFFICIAL by assertion; a foreign tenant's feed is invisible.

P13-T06 Conformal core
- Files: `kernel/lib/conformal/`.
- Steps: split conformal prediction with class-conditional calibration (Part 3 §3.13), calibration artifacts versioned and reproducible, empty or oversized sets produce ABSTAIN, rolling coverage monitor that disables automatic use on a real-data drop beyond tolerance (it never disables anything on SYNTHETIC events or on NOT_MEASURABLE, and reports a VALIDATED_TENANT artifact that has stayed NOT_MEASURABLE beyond the revalidation period so the class can lapse); coverage statements carry SYNTHETIC unless real calibration data is registered; every calibration artifact has a validation class (default CALIBRATED_SYNTHETIC); implement the tenant validation of Part 5 §5.1.6a as pure functions (Wilson bounds, the one-member subset bound, the pass criterion read from the capability configuration, lapse on a changed pack version, artifact or labelled set); the class changes only through the recorded activation, never inside the library.
- Accept: tests T-LIB-0063 to T-LIB-0076: empirical coverage over at least 1,000 seeded trials meets the bound; outputs are deterministic for fixed seeds; the monitor disables use on a seeded drop; the guarantee text states marginal coverage only; validation passes on a seeded labelled set that meets the criterion and fails on one that does not, including a set whose one-member subset error is too high while the overall coverage is fine; the class lapses on a changed pack version; no library function sets VALIDATED_TENANT without the recorded activation.

P13-T07 Solver harness and independent verifier scaffold
- Files: `kernel/lib/solve/`.
- Steps: CP-SAT and Z3 harnesses with fixed seeds and thread counts, time limits, and a model-encoding interface; every solution is re-checked by an independent verifier (a separate plain-code checker of the constraints, not the solver) and an infeasibility claim is accompanied by a Z3 or recorded reason; solver results are labelled as holding relative to the encoded model.
- Accept: tests T-LIB-0077 to T-LIB-0090: a seeded wrong solution is caught by the verifier; results are stable across thread counts for fixed seeds or the instability is reported and the model rejected; time limit expiry yields a recorded ABSTAIN.

P13-T08 Lexicographic stability helper and cross-check helper
- Files: `kernel/lib/lexi/`, `kernel/lib/crosscheck/`.
- Steps: the lexicographic objective helper that fixes ties by explicit keys so equal inputs give one plan (for L06 and L08); the model cross-check helper that runs two independent extractions through the Gateway and returns agreement or HOLD with both citations (for K14 and L09); both are pure interfaces over earlier modules.
- Accept: tests T-LIB-0091 to T-LIB-0100: permuting the input order does not change the lexicographic result; disagreement gives HOLD; agreement is recorded with both model identifiers.

P13-T09 Differential suites and traceability extension
- Files: `test-house/lib/differential/`, `tools/trace/` extension.
- Steps: the differential tests named in Part 6 §6.3 and Part 5 batch E §E0 for each module; extend the traceability tool to cover module-level requirements and the shared-module table of Part 6 §6.10.
- Accept: tests T-LIB-0101 to T-LIB-0108: every module in the shared-module table has a differential or known-answer suite; `gate run trace differential` exit 0.

P13-RISK Risk review per Part 6 §6.5: controls for rounding errors, label inflation, solver misreport, miscalibrated confidence, feed forgery, unstable plans; update the registers; `reviews/P13.md`.

P13-END1, P13-END2: per Part 1 §10.

7. Test plan. No capability. No new gate; `differential` and `trace` extended. Tier A: unit and property tests; Tier B: differential, solver and coverage trials. Test ids T-LIB-0001 to T-LIB-0112.

8. Risk review: task P13-RISK.

9. Exit conditions beyond Part 1 §9: all gates exit 0; every module of the table in Part 6 §6.10 is built and tested; the feed envelope and the capability layout are registered in `contracts.lock`; the stub capability runs through the skeleton; risk review filed; README ticked by the gate.

10. Look-ahead.

- P15 and later phases add each capability to the skeleton; none of them copies a module from this phase.

P14-T01 Intake stage and Document Reader interface
- Files: `kernel/intake/`, `adapters/readers/`.
- Steps: store original bytes under SHA-256; detect type, language and script; NFC text and ASCII digit mapping with both forms recorded; minimal-PII handling before any log; readers sit behind the Document Reader interface for the formats of the Intake registry (ASSUMPTION: text-bearing PDF, DOCX, XLSX, CSV, plain text; scanned images only through the optional scan reader of P14-T13, otherwise TEXT_UNAVAILABLE as K14 T6 states); reader metadata (producer, creation and modification times, revision count) is recorded for K09; the stage writes Document records and ledger events through the P04 and P05 interfaces.
- Accept: tests T-INT-0001 to T-INT-0020: a document round-trips by hash; both digit forms are retained; a scanned image without a scan reader yields no text with a recorded reason; reader metadata is stored; a tenant cannot read another tenant's document.

P14-T02 Sandboxed parsers and hostile-file defences
- Files: `adapters/readers/sandbox/`, `tools/threat-matrix/` mappings.
- Steps: every parser runs in a subprocess with CPU, memory, size and time limits and no network; decompression bombs, malformed and deeply nested files, oversized inputs, zip path tricks and embedded active content are refused or neutralized and recorded; map the hostile-file item of the threat catalog (deferred from P11).
- Accept: tests T-INT-0021 to T-INT-0040: each hostile sample ends in a recorded refusal within its limits; a parser crash never crashes the stage; the mapped threat item has tests.

P14-T03 Text normalization functions and K14 pack
- Files: `kernel/lib/text/`, `capabilities/k14/packs/`.
- Steps: shared functions for the comparison form (NFKC, tatweel and diacritics removed, alef and yeh folding per pack, zero-width and bidirectional controls removed, whitespace collapsed), digit and separator mapping, unit-name mapping across Arabic and English, date patterns with month names in both languages; the Document Normalization Pack with sources recorded and label DERIVED; Hijri conversion only through an optionally registered Calendar Conversion Pack (V-06).
- Accept: tests T-K14-0201 to T-K14-0230 (property, metamorphic): normalization is idempotent; digit mapping round-trips with the recorded originals; adding tatweel, diacritics or direction controls leaves normalized facts unchanged; confusable letters are not merged.

P14-T04 K14 extraction, verification and flags
- Files: `capabilities/k14/`.
- Steps: language and script profile with the 60 percent supported-share rule; at most 2 model calls per document through the Gateway in tool-less, secret-less mode; deterministic grounding (quote is a substring of the comparison form at the locator, numbers and dates re-parse, identifiers match patterns) with failures dropped to UNKNOWN; flags MIXED_DIGITS, AMBIGUOUS_DATE, HIJRI_UNCONVERTED, OTHER_LANGUAGE_PART; NAME_LINK proposals that are never equality until confirmed; parallel-document check; status codes of K14 T6.
- Accept: tests T-K14-0101 to T-K14-0111 (one per status code) and T-K14-0001 to T-K14-0020 (scenarios K14-S01 to S20); a value absent from the text never becomes a fact; model unavailable gives ABSTAIN, MODEL_UNAVAILABLE; the budget code is reachable.

P14-T05 K14 interfaces, evidence and tests
- Files: `capabilities/k14/` (route registration, view, evidence), `data/scenarios/k14/`, `test-house/k14/`.
- Steps: uniform route and command; logical-order view with original and normalized forms side by side and right-to-left isolation; evidence records with model identifier, prompt hash and response hash and replay from recordings; the parallel corpus of SYNTHETIC Arabic and English documents covering every field type (PR-11 test); extraction quality measured on SYNTHETIC data and reported with that label.
- Accept: tests T-K14-0301 to T-K14-0330 (bidirectional tricks, mixed digits, look-alikes, long lines, injection text in either language), T-K14-0401 to T-K14-0410 (replay, tenant isolation), T-K14-0501 to T-K14-0512 (view stub registration, budget, responsible-use assertions); the differential parallel-corpus test passes.

P14-T06 F01 pack, comparators and arithmetic
- Files: `capabilities/f01/`.
- Steps: agreement sets and the comparators NAME, EXACT, SET, QUANTITY, MONEY, PREFIX, TOKENS, DATE over normalized values; constraints ARI-1 to ARI-6 in exact decimal arithmetic through the money module; the legal-form suffix pack labelled DERIVED; the container check-digit rule registered and enabled from the Source Registry entry of V-10 part a; required document set as workflow configuration.
- Accept: tests T-F01-0201 to T-F01-0240 (property, metamorphic): report independent of document and fact order; kilograms to grams leaves agreement unchanged; adding irrelevant facts changes nothing; adding a contradicting verified fact never lowers severity; QUANTITY agreement over three or more documents is order-independent and equals non-empty common intersection; ARI-3 and ARI-5 interval sums match a brute-force enumeration on generated cases.

P14-T07 F01 Z3 core, canonical facts and outcomes
- Files: `capabilities/f01/`.
- Steps: encode the same constraints for Z3 through the solver harness; use it for the differential test and for the minimal unsatisfiable core cited in findings; disagreement yields ESCALATE with F01.ESCALATE.SOLVER_DISAGREEMENT; request canonical shipment facts through the Kernel write path (VERIFIED, DERIVED, source = agreeing fact ids) only for fully agreeing sets; combine by the most severe finding; reason codes of F01 T6 closed.
- Accept: tests T-F01-0101 to T-F01-0126 (one per reason code) and T-F01-0001 to T-F01-0012 (scenarios F01-S01 to S12); the decimal evaluator and Z3 agree on generated constraint sets and the core is minimal; no canonical fact is produced for a disagreeing set.

P14-T08 F01 interfaces, evidence and tests
- Files: `capabilities/f01/`, `data/scenarios/f01/`, `test-house/f01/`.
- Steps: ConsistencyReport with graph, findings and template explanations; view with documents as columns and agreement sets as rows (icon and text, never colour alone); evidence per Part 5 T8 and replay to the same output hash; adversarial corpus (instruction text in fields, confusable and zero-width characters, mixed direction, large nested documents, duplicate fields).
- Accept: tests T-F01-0301 to T-F01-0330, T-F01-0401 to T-F01-0410, T-F01-0501 to T-F01-0512; no output proposes a corrected value (CP7); replay of every scenario matches the output hash.

P14-T09 K09 fingerprint index and Document Identity Pack
- Files: `capabilities/k09/`, tenant-scoped fingerprint tables under the P04 lint.
- Steps: per-tenant fingerprints at intake (content hash, normalized-text hash, MinHash of 5-token shingles with 128 permutations and fixed seeds, identity-key hash from VERIFIED identity facts); bucket index on MinHash bands with exact Jaccard verification and the 1,000-candidate fail-closed cap; Document Identity Pack labelled DERIVED; fingerprints follow the erasure of their document.
- Accept: tests T-K09-0201 to T-K09-0230 (property, differential): MinHash search against brute-force exact Jaccard misses no pair at or above the lower band; similarity is symmetric; adding an unrelated document changes no finding; tenant isolation of the index.

P14-T10 K09 findings, relations and symmetry
- Files: `capabilities/k09/`.
- Steps: same-case observation, reuse, identity conflict (protective matching mode), near duplicate, corroborated lower band, metadata signals as observations only; reviewer-declared relations as VERIFIED link facts that apply only to the pair named; symmetry: the other open case's result becomes STALE and is recomputed (5.1.8); other-case name shown only to users who may read it; reason codes of K09 T6 closed.
- Accept: tests T-K09-0101 to T-K09-0113 (one per code) and T-K09-0001 to T-K09-0017 (scenarios K09-S01 to S17); the symmetry test shows both cases after a reuse; the access test hides the other case from a viewer without access.

P14-T11 K09 interfaces, evidence and tests
- Files: `capabilities/k09/`, `data/scenarios/k09/`, `test-house/k09/`.
- Steps: DocumentShieldReport with coverage record; view with side-by-side differing fields and the relation declaration action; evidence without document text in the ledger; adversarial corpus (homoglyph identity keys, zero-width insertions, shingle-breaking noise, huge documents, crafted buckets); erasure test.
- Accept: tests T-K09-0301 to T-K09-0330, T-K09-0401 to T-K09-0412, T-K09-0501 to T-K09-0512; the erasure test removes the fingerprint with its document; no output explains how to evade detection (CP7).

P14-T12 Skeleton integration, pair tests and hardening

- Steps: the skeleton now starts from a real document set through Intake, K14 and F01 and passes K09; pair tests: K14 output feeds F01 across languages without a false contradiction, F01 canonical facts are absent when K14 flags a value, K09 reuse across two cases marks the earlier case STALE; run mutation-guided hardening (CP5) on the three capabilities; generate capability cards and the Definition of Done tables (CP4, CP8).
- Accept: tests T-INT-0041 to T-INT-0060 (pairs and skeleton); `gate run mutation trace e2e` exit 0; every surviving mutant is killed or recorded as equivalent; the three capabilities tick in the status map.

P14-T13 Optional scan reader
- Files: `adapters/readers/scan/`, `data/scenarios/k14/`, `test-house/intake/`.
- Steps: a Scan Reader implementation of the Document Reader interface that runs the OCR executable named in `docs/decisions/run-parameters.md` inside the sandbox of P14-T02 (no network, CPU, memory, size and time limits); before first use it checks the executable's reported version and the SHA-256 of the executable and of the Arabic and English language data files against the values the owner recorded (V-31), and on any mismatch or absence the reader stays disabled and documents yield TEXT_UNAVAILABLE; it returns text with per-line confidence and page and box locators and never writes a fact; the agent never downloads or installs the engine; SYNTHETIC English scan fixtures are produced with the standard library only (seeded skew and noise), and the Arabic scan fixtures are supplied by the owner under V-31, until which Arabic scan reading is reported UNPROVEN and every Arabic OCR fact stays PROPOSED; digit and character error rates are measured per script on the fixtures and reported with the corpus label.
- Accept: tests T-INT-0061 to T-INT-0080: disabled reader and tampered executable or language data give TEXT_UNAVAILABLE; a hostile image ends in a recorded refusal within its limits; OCR text never produces a VERIFIED fact without a recorded reviewer confirmation; a low-confidence line leaves its fields UNKNOWN; the per-script error report is generated.

P14-RISK Risk review per Part 6 §6.5: controls for hostile files, false contradictions caused by language, ungrounded extraction, silent value correction, document reuse missed, cross-tenant fingerprint leakage, solver disagreement; update the registers; `reviews/P14.md`.

P15-T01 K05 valuation basis and reference statistics
- Files: `kernel/lib/valuation/`, `capabilities/k05/`.
- Steps: the valuation basis function (invoice total converted at the FX Pack rate for the declared date plus the adjustments the Valuation Rule Pack requires for the incoterm; missing required adjustment abstains) as a shared module with a differential suite; unit value in the reference basis through the unit table; reference statistics m_c and s_c (median of ln unit value and 1.4826 times MAD with floor s_min 0.05), parent-level fallback recorded, abstain below a sample of 30.
- Accept: tests T-K05-0201 to T-K05-0230 (property, differential): unit and currency conversions that are exact leave results unchanged; quantile, median and MAD equal reference implementations; the basis function equals an independent decimal implementation.

P15-T02 K05 conformal interval, outcomes and visibility
- Files: `capabilities/k05/`.
- Steps: nonconformity a = |ln v − m_c| / s_c; split-conformal q̂ from at least 500 calibration draws bound to the Reference Pack version hash (alpha 0.10); closed interval; per-code aggregate across lines of the same code so splitting cannot hide a low average; interval bounds shown only to reviewer and administrator roles; the coverage claim verbatim from T4a; diagnostics (held-out and shifted) archived; reason codes of K05 T6 closed.
- Accept: tests T-K05-0001 to T-K05-0015 (scenarios K05-S01 to S15), T-K05-0101 to T-K05-0114; the 1,000-trial coverage test is within 3 standard errors of 90 percent and carries the SYNTHETIC label; the access test hides bounds from non-reviewers.

P15-T03 K05 interfaces, adversarial and responsible use
- Files: `capabilities/k05/`, `data/scenarios/k05/`, `test-house/k05/`.
- Steps: route registration, view with the banner "advisory plausibility check, not a valuation ruling", evidence records; adversarial corpus (zero or negative values, extreme reference outliers, unit confusion, tiny quantities); no output states what the value should be or what would pass (CP7).
- Accept: tests T-K05-0301 to T-K05-0320, T-K05-0401 to T-K05-0410, T-K05-0501 to T-K05-0512.

P15-T04 F02 scorer and calibration
- Files: `capabilities/f02/`, `data/synthetic/calibration/`.
- Steps: model-free scorer (BM25 form with pack parameters, digits discarded so a code typed in a description has no effect, normalized by the candidate's self-score) giving nonconformity 1 − sim; two-layer calibration set (pack text variants plus a separate commercial-name lexicon not derived from tariff texts) of at least max(500, 10 × C) pairs, C being the number of chapters of the pack, with every chapter at least 10 times, bound to the tariff pack version hash; shifted validation set used only for the diagnostic; per-language calibration and the optional closed glossary of item b2 of T4 with deterministic, recorded token expansion (a SYNTHETIC test glossary only; real glossary content is pack authoring); candidate set, set-size rule 3, rule filters applied only on VERIFIED applicability facts and recorded.
- Accept: tests T-F02-0201 to T-F02-0234 (property, differential, statistical): similarity in [0, 1]; smaller alpha gives a superset; the optimized index equals a reference scorer; the quantile equals its brute-force definition; coverage within 3 standard errors over 1,000 seeded trials; a pack without a glossary abstains on Arabic descriptions; with the test glossary the expansion is identical on repeated runs; a language without its own bound calibration set abstains; a seeded pack with 96 chapters and a 500-pair set abstains with CALIBRATION_STALE, a 960-pair set with 10 per chapter passes, a chapter with 9 pairs is refused; the size requirement changes with the chapter count of the pack and the artifact records C.

P15-T05 F02 outcomes, interfaces and tests
- Files: `capabilities/f02/`, `data/scenarios/f02/`, `test-house/f02/`.
- Steps: validity checks at the evaluation instant (including the last valid instant), resolution level when the declared code is deeper than the scorer resolves, per-line decision and the case outcome as the most severe line; verbatim coverage claim and banner "advisory consistency check, not an official classification"; reason codes of F02 T6 closed; adversarial corpus (keyword stuffing, copied headings, mixed scripts, very long descriptions); no output ranks candidates by duty or advises rewording (CP7).
- Accept: tests T-F02-0001 to T-F02-0012 (scenarios F02-S01 to S12), T-F02-0101 to T-F02-0114, T-F02-0301 to T-F02-0320, T-F02-0401 to T-F02-0410, T-F02-0501 to T-F02-0512; S11 and S12 run as the statistical test and the diagnostic with no pass/fail on the nominal level; the report of S11 carries the label "implementation-correctness" and never the words "validated" or "real data" (claims lexicon, P7-CLAIMS). Tests T-F02-0231 to T-F02-0234: on a seeded shifted set (changed description style or chapter mix) the share of ABSTAIN and ESCALATE results is at least the share on the unshifted set plus a stated margin and never lower than it; a shift never raises the ALLOW share; a shifted set from a pack older than its calibration abstains with CALIBRATION_STALE; the S11 report without its label fails.

P15-T06 Skeleton integration, pair tests and hardening
- Files: `kernel/skeleton/`, `test-house/pairs/`.
- Steps: K05 and F02 added to the skeleton as Decision inputs; pair tests: both read F01 canonical facts and abstain when they are missing; a changed line description marks both results STALE (5.1.8); label propagation caps ALLOW when a pack is SECONDARY; mutation-guided hardening (CP5), cards and Definition of Done tables (CP4, CP8).
- Accept: tests T-VAL-0001 to T-VAL-0014; `gate run mutation trace e2e` exit 0; both capabilities tick.

P15-RISK Risk review per Part 6 §6.5: controls for overstated confidence, learnable acceptance range, miscalibration under shift, expired reference data, unit and currency confusion; update the registers; `reviews/P15.md`.

P15-END1, P15-END2: per Part 1 §10.

7. Test plan. Capabilities K05 (K05-S01 to S15) and F02 (F02-S01 to S12): every scenario maps to the golden test with the same number (CP2, CP3). New gates: none. Tier A: unit and property tests; Tier B: statistical trials, differential, pair tests, mutation by impact. Test ids: T-VAL-0001 to T-VAL-0014, T-K05-0001 to T-K05-0599, T-F02-0001 to T-F02-0599.

8. Risk review: task P15-RISK.

9. Exit conditions beyond Part 1 §9: all gates exit 0; coverage statements carry the SYNTHETIC label everywhere; both capabilities meet the twelve Definition of Done items; the valuation basis function is exported for L02; output schemas registered in `contracts.lock`; README capability marks ticked; risk review filed.

10. Look-ahead.
- P16 (L02) imports the valuation basis function from this phase; do not rename its interface without a change task.

11. Budget: class M; retries 3; tool calls 400; token budget named in the owner's command.

PHASE 16 — CLOCKS, CHARGES AND LANDED COST

Everything else is forbidden.

6. Tasks.

P16-T01 F03 clock engine
- Files: `kernel/lib/clock/`, `capabilities/f03/`.
- Steps: start and stop instants from events; day boundaries in the tariff time zone; day-one rule, cut-off, partial-day rule (a missing rule abstains, no default); CALENDAR and WORKING bases through the calendar pack; VERIFIED suspension windows excluded and shown; tiered charge in fixed point with the terms' rounding mode; remaining free time, state (STOPPED, ACCRUING, ENDING with the 48-hour window, WITHIN_FREE, UNKNOWN); projections at +1, +3 and +7 days; action options with latest effective time and savings; aggregates per currency with no conversion; a second implementation as a day-by-day simulation.
- Accept: tests T-F03-0201 to T-F03-0235 (property, differential, metamorphic): charge is non-decreasing in evaluation time and zero before expiry; an earlier stop or an added suspension window never increases the charge; splitting a period and summing gives the same charge; closed form equals the day-by-day simulation over generated terms, events and suspensions; daylight-saving and leap-day cases follow the zone rules.

P16-T02 F03 outcomes, interfaces and tests
- Files: `capabilities/f03/`, `data/scenarios/f03/`, `test-house/f03/`.
- Steps: container matching against the F01 canonical set; event order checks; case outcome as the most severe container state; reason codes of F03 T6 closed; timeline view with bars and text; adversarial corpus (extension claims in text, impossible dates, overlapping suspension windows, huge periods); no output suggests changing or backdating an event (CP7).
- Accept: tests T-F03-0001 to T-F03-0012 (scenarios F03-S01 to S12), T-F03-0101 to T-F03-0111, T-F03-0301 to T-F03-0320, T-F03-0401 to T-F03-0410, T-F03-0501 to T-F03-0512; a text claim of an extension has no effect unless a VERIFIED term.

P16-T03 L01 taxonomy, basis matching and line classes
- Files: `kernel/lib/taxonomy/`, `capabilities/l01/`.
- Steps: charge taxonomy mapping (English and Arabic synonyms) as the one shared module; line kinds TIME_BASED, TAX, OTHER; basis candidates (accepted quotation, contract terms, published tariff) with default precedence and the AMBIGUOUS and UNQUOTED classes; amount check in exact decimals at the currency's minor unit with tolerance 0; classes in the stated order (DUPLICATE, POSSIBLE_DUPLICATE with band 2 percent and band 0 off, UNQUOTED, AMBIGUOUS, CURRENCY_MISMATCH, SURCHARGE_OUT_OF_VALIDITY, QUANTITY_MISMATCH, RATE_ABOVE_BASIS, ARITHMETIC, FREIGHT_TERMS_CONFLICT, MATCH, BELOW_BASIS); invoice-level HEADER_TOTAL check.
- Accept: tests T-L01-0201 to T-L01-0235 (property, differential, metamorphic): a corrected invoice yields ALLOW; classification is independent of line order; adding a duplicate line never lowers severity; widening the near-duplicate band never removes a flag; unit conversion of quantities and synonym substitution through the taxonomy leave outcomes unchanged; decimal arithmetic equals an independent implementation.

P16-T04 L01 outcomes, match records and tests
- Files: `capabilities/l01/`, `data/scenarios/l01/`, `test-house/l01/`.
- Steps: outcome mapping (HOLD, ESCALATE, ABSTAIN, ALLOW, most severe wins); match records for every TIME_BASED line published for F07; totals per currency (billed, expected, difference); trace of each expected amount (E0.2) and unblock requirements (E0.3); reason codes of L01 T6 closed; adversarial corpus (look-alike charge names, negative lines, overlapping validity, split duplicates with small differences, malformed rows); no output drafts a dispute or advises withholding payment (CP7).
- Accept: tests T-L01-0001 to T-L01-0018 (scenarios L01-S01 to S18), T-L01-0101 to T-L01-0120, T-L01-0301 to T-L01-0325, T-L01-0401 to T-L01-0410, T-L01-0501 to T-L01-0512.

P16-T05 L02 components, duty and tax
- Files: `capabilities/l02/`.
- Steps: valuation basis from the shared function of P15 (a differential test against K05 on generated cases); duty per line under the declared code only (AD_VALOREM, SPECIFIC, COMPOUND); a preferential rate only when the pack rule holds on VERIFIED facts, otherwise the standard rate with a note; taxes on the pack-defined base; component origin kinds DOCUMENT, PACK and ASSUMPTION with the weakest label carried to the total; per-component currency, reporting-currency totals through the FX Pack with rate, date and label; unconverted components mark the total INCOMPLETE; min, base and max by decimal interval arithmetic; unit costs when quantities are VERIFIED; a partial sum is never shown as the total.
- Accept: tests T-L02-0201 to T-L02-0235 (property, differential, metamorphic): total equals the sum of components; min ≤ base ≤ max; scaling all prices scales ad valorem duty by the same factor; unit conversion leaves specific duty unchanged; duty and tax equal an independent decimal implementation.

P16-T06 L02 variance, simulation use and tests
- Files: `capabilities/l02/`, `data/scenarios/l02/`, `test-house/l02/`.
- Steps: variance section from L01 audited lines (actual minus estimate, percentage, UNESTIMATED list, observation only, published for L10); evaluation on a proposed F05 plan with leg costs replacing the recorded transport components, result SIMULATED and written to the simulation record kind only; F02's second-candidate codes never used; traces and unblock requirements; reason codes of L02 T6 closed; adversarial corpus (zero and negative values, huge quantities, overlapping rate windows, currency without minor-unit data); no output suggests changing a declaration to lower duty (CP7).
- Accept: tests T-L02-0001 to T-L02-0017 (scenarios L02-S01 to S17), T-L02-0101 to T-L02-0111, T-L02-0301 to T-L02-0320, T-L02-0401 to T-L02-0410, T-L02-0501 to T-L02-0512.

P16-T07 F07 line audit and dossier
- Files: `capabilities/f07/`.
- Steps: in-scope lines are those L01 matched as TIME_BASED; flat fees pass to L01 and TAX lines are reported as not evaluated; expected charge recomputed with the F03 engine under the matched basis; comparison at the minor unit with tolerance 0; classification in the stated order (DUPLICATE, NOT_BILLABLE_PERIOD, UNSUPPORTED, AMBIGUOUS_TARIFF, RATE_MISMATCH, DAY_COUNT_MISMATCH, ARITHMETIC, MATCH, UNDERCHARGE) with root cause as the first explaining check; outcome mapping; dossier (JSON and printable HTML, hash-bound) from fixed templates stating discrepancies only, labelled INDICATIVE when its weakest label is below DERIVED.
- Accept: tests T-F07-0201 to T-F07-0225 (property, differential, metamorphic): expected-charge recomputation equals the F03 engine for the same inputs; classification is independent of line order; a corrected invoice yields ALLOW; adding a VERIFIED free-time extension never increases over-billing differences; the root-cause order equals a table-driven reference; the same inputs give the same dossier hash.

P16-T08 F07 tests and adversarial corpus
- Files: `data/scenarios/f07/`, `test-house/f07/`.
- Steps: reason codes of F07 T6 closed; adversarial corpus (overlapping periods, negative amounts, currency mismatch, duplicated lines with tiny differences); dossier preview and export; no output states that money is owed (CP7).
- Accept: tests T-F07-0001 to T-F07-0011 (scenarios F07-S01 to S11), T-F07-0101 to T-F07-0113, T-F07-0301 to T-F07-0320, T-F07-0401 to T-F07-0410, T-F07-0501 to T-F07-0512.

P16-T09 Skeleton integration, pair tests and hardening
- Files: `kernel/skeleton/`, `test-house/pairs/`.
- Steps: the four capabilities added to the skeleton; pair tests: L01 publishes match records that F07 reads and F07 judges the amount; F03 and F07 agree on the same clock; L02 total equals the sum of L02 components and its basis equals K05's; a corrected invoice flows ALLOW through L01 and F07; a changed tariff version marks F03, L01 and F07 results STALE (5.1.8); mutation-guided hardening (CP5), cards and Definition of Done tables (CP4, CP8).
- Accept: tests T-FIN-0001 to T-FIN-0016; `gate run mutation trace e2e perf` exit 0; the four capabilities tick.

P16-RISK Risk review per Part 6 §6.5: controls for wrong day counting, calendar errors, money rounding drift, duplicate or double-counted charges, preferential rate misuse, estimate shown as final; update the registers; `reviews/P16.md`.

P16-END1, P16-END2: per Part 1 §10.
